---

> Cikakken bayani na kowane environment variable da OmniRoute ke ganewa.
> Don samfurin farawa cikin sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Dole ne kowane variable da aka rubuta a nan ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma
> kowane variable da ke cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> yana tabbatar da wannan yayin commit da kuma a cikin CI. Don tsallake wani variable da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa
> allowlist da ke cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirrukan da Ake Buƙata

Sirrai da ba a saita ba waɗanda aka alama da **Eh** ana ƙirƙira su ta atomatik a farkon gwudanarwa (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) kuma a adana su a cikin `server.env` a babban kundin bayanai — saita su a bayyane idan wannan kundin na ɗan gajeren lokaci ne. Keɓance ita ce `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ba a adana shi a cikin `server.env` ba — shigarwar uwar garken (entrypoints) na ƙirƙira ƙima ta ɗan lokaci a kowane boots wanda gada cikin tsari kawai za ta iya amfani da shi, saboda haka abokan ciniki na wajen gada na buƙatar a saita shi a bayyane.

| Sauyi                        | Ana Buƙata          | Tsoho                           | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------- | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Eh**              | _(babu)_                        | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis na zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Eh**              | _(babu)_                        | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Maɓallin ɓoyewar AES don ƙimomin maɓallan API da aka adana a SQLite. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | A'a                 | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Yana saita kalmar sirrin dashboard lokacin farawa har yanzu babu wanda aka ajiye; ana hunfa da shi da zarar an ajiye kalmar sirri. npm da shigarwa daga tushen code na kwafi `.env.example` zuwa `.env`, don haka suna farawa da placeholder ɗinsa da aka sani a fili `CHANGEME`: maye shi kafin farkon boots, ko saita `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (mudu) a cikin wannan `.env` domin a ƙirƙiri kalmar sirri a cikin onboarding wizard a ziyarar farko, kamar yadda ƙimar da ba a saita ba (tsohuwar Docker image) ke yi. Sauya kalmar sirri da aka ajiye a Dashboard → Settings → Security, ko ka gudanar da `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` a cikin source checkout) da `DATA_DIR` da aka saita zuwa babban kundin bayanan uwar garken. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Eh** (production) | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin haɗin gwiwa na gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin Electron/browser WS relay da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUƘATARSA a production — idan ba a saita shi ba, za a ƙi duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a                 | _(git SHA)_                     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | ID na musamman na kawar da tsohon cache na service-worker don PWA shell (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin tantancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a                 | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit ɗin da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a                 | _(an samo)_                     | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin ginawa da client ke amfani da ita wajen rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (ta atomatik)   | _(ta atomatik a kowane farawa)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin kowane process da ke tabbatar da cewa amintaccen tambarin peer-IP ya fito daga HTTP server na OmniRoute kansa (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). authz middleware yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata a gida (loopback/LAN gating na hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY) ne kawai idan tambarin yana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana ƙirƙira shi ta atomatik a kowane farawa — bar shi ba a saita ba; saita shi kawai don tsarin multi-process da dole ne su raba tambarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | A'a                 | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IPs ko CIDR ranges na reverse proxies da aka raba da waƙafi, waɗanda jerin yarda/ƙin IP zai iya amincewa da `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nasu, ban da loopback, adiresoshin private-network da Cloudflare edges, waɗanda a koyaushe ake amincewa da su. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don proxy da ke kan wani adiresoshin jama'a dabam; idan ba haka ba, adiresoshin proxy ɗin kansa ne filter zai tantance.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Umarnin Ƙirƙirawa

```bash
# Ƙirƙiri dukkan sirruka huɗu lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada a taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu ɗauke da sirruka na gaske zuwa version control. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma tabbatar kafin pushing.

---

## 2. Ma’ajiya da Rumbun Bayanai

OmniRoute yana amfani da **SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) don duk wani adana bayanai na dindindin. Waɗannan sauye-sauye suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayowar rayuwarsu.

| Sauyi                                      | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Babban kundin adireshi na SQLite DB, madadin bayanai, da fayilolin bayanai. Sauya shi don kundin Docker ko hanyoyi na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Hanyar kauce wa kariyar DATA_DIR ta gwaji/eval (#10428). Ana karkatar da gwaje-gwaje da binciken eval/print na Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, ciki har da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=`) waɗanda ba su da `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin wucin gadi da za a yar, domin kada su buɗe ainihin rumbun bayanan mai gudanarwa; saita zuwa `1` don sake amincewa da amfani da ainihin kundin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na artifact ɗin da ke gudana. Ana hatimce shi ta `npm run build:release`; ana iya shigar da shi cikin containers da ake turawa ba tare da alamar `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Ana nuna shi a matsayin `system.buildSha` a `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref da ƙofar tabbatar da asalin pack-artifact ke kwatanta build SHA da shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar haɗa build wanda SHA ɗinsa ba ya kan layin fitarwa, tare da rikodin sa a matsayin canary da aka yi da gangan maimakon ƙofar ta hana shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Maɓallin API don binciken smoke na tura canary, ana aika shi a matsayin `Authorization: Bearer` zuwa `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun tura tsarin ne kawai ke amfani da shi (#10429), uwar garken ba ta taɓa amfani da shi. Ba shi da alaƙa da sauye-sauyen `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na tsarin gwajin smoke na CLI wanda sai an amince a kunna shi (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [Haɗin CLI → Ainihin zagayen gwajin smoke](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Alamar matakin build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` yana saita ta zuwa `1`, sannan duk wani build worker da aka ƙaddamar yana gadonta, domin layin DB ya mayar da stub marar aiki maimakon loda native addon na better-sqlite3 (wanda ke dakatar da worker lokacin fita). Ba a taɓa saita ta ga uwar garken da ke gudana ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken prebuild na native-dependency na zaɓi don bishiyoyin vendored na musamman. Wannan ba ya sa dependency da ya ɓace ya zama abin ginawa; yi amfani da shi ne kawai idan ana samar da native dependencies ta wata hanya dabam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Laƙabin madadin** na `DATA_DIR`, ana duba shi ne kawai idan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` ba. Ana amfani da shi don gano ma’ajiyar zaman thread-sticky ta PromptQL executor da ke kan faifai (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan ba a saita ko ɗaya daga cikin sauye-sauyen ba, ma’ajiyar tana kasancewa cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai (ba a adana ta tsakanin sake kunnawa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Kundin adireshin da **runtime plugin scanner** ke karantawa — kuma tushen da plugin manager ke girkawa a ciki — yana maye gurbin tsohon wurin da ake samo daga home (#11827). Nuna shi zuwa bishiyar plugin da aka bind-mount a Docker/K8s maimakon matsar da HOME kawai don canja wurin hanyar binciken (HOME yana sarrafa duk sauran halayen da suka danganci home ma). Idan ba a saita ba = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` idan process bai fitar da wani home kwata-kwata ba — wannan sauyin yana kawar da rashin gano plugins ba tare da sanarwa ba. Ana rubuta kundin da aka warware sau ɗaya lokacin farawa a matsayin `scanner.dir_resolved`, tare da shigarwar da aka zaɓa. Na ɓangaren uwar garke kawai: plugins na umarnin CLI suna ci gaba da amfani da nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(babu komai = an kashe)_     | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoye bayanan SQLite lokacin da suke a ma'ajiya. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar maɓallin ɓoyewa. Ƙara ta yayin sauya maɓalli domin tallafa wa warware ɓoyewar tsofaffin kwafin ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Idan `true`, ana tsallake kwafin ajiyar fayil ɗin SQLite na yau da kullum/kafin rubutu (adana/share farashin models.dev, rubuta saituna). Kwafin ajiya na hannu da na kafin maidowa har yanzu suna gudana. Wannan **ba ya** kashe tilas ɗin ɗaukar tabbataccen hoton kariya na mai gudanar da ƙaura ko kariyar ƙaura mai yawa ga DB mai ɗorewa da ya riga ya wanzu. Ana iyakance kwafin ajiyar da ba na hannu ba zuwa sau ɗaya kacal cikin mintuna 60. **Settings → Storage** na dashboard na iya kashe kwafin ajiya na yau da kullum kai tsaye dabam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon suna na madadi** don `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ana karɓarsa a matsayin madadi idan babban canjin bai wanzu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon suna na madadi** (nau'in da aka yi wa ɓoyewar Base64) wanda ake karɓa a matsayin madadi. Ana warware shi ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Sauya tazarar binciken lafiyar SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Idan ba a saita ba, ana samo tsoffin ƙimomi daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire.** Yin `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` na lokaci-lokaci yayin da tsarin ke aiki na iya lalata taswirar wal-index da ake rabawa, ya kuma sa tsarin ya rushe da SIGBUS (#13973), saboda haka mai tsara lokacin ba ya wanzu. Canjin ba ya aiki: ƙima mai kyau tana rubuta gargaɗin daina amfani sau ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saitawa ba ya fitar da komai. Ana kula da WAL ta wuraren dubawa na PASSIVE (a ƙasa), sannan wurin dubawa na rufewa yana datse shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (minti 5)            | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Sauya tazarar `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` mai yawan faruwa (ms). Yana rage girman firam ɗin WAL da ke jira domin wuraren dubawa su kasance masu sauri, kuma fayil ɗin WAL ya kasance cikin iyaka tsakanin datsewar lokacin rufewa. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Idan zagayen PASSIVE ya tarar da fayil ɗin WAL ya wuce wannan girman, a gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` domin WAL ya fara daga farko ba tare da sake rubuta wal-index da aka tsara ba. An cire wuraren dubawa na yanayin datsewa yayin aiki (duba layin `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin fita daga tsarin bayan matsanancin matsi na albarkatu ya ci gaba har tsawon `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, don bai wa mai sa ido (systemd `Restart=always`, manufar sake kunnawa ta Docker) damar dawo da tsari mai tsabta maimakon ci gaba da bayar da 503s har abada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (minti 2)            | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tsawon lokacin da matsanancin matsi dole ne ya ci gaba kafin fitawar sake kunnawa ta kai ta fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin yin watsi da `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) na dukkan host lokacin da maƙwabci mai hayaniya ya haddasa 503s alhali wannan tsarin yana lafiya (#13124). Kariyoyin cgroup da V8 heap za su ci gaba da aiki. Haka kuma PSI zai daina ƙidayuwa a matsayin hujjar takurawar kernel ga `cgroup_high`: jimillar cgroup da ta haura `memory.high` za ta zama mai haɗari ne kawai idan `high`/`max` na `memory.events` ya ƙaru. Idan babu `memory.events`, ana riƙe ɗanyen rabon.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Takamaiman hanyar fayil (cikakkiya ko mai danganta da cwd) zuwa `sql-wasm.wasm` lokacin amfani da adaftan madadin WASM na `sql.js`. Idan ba a saita ba, ana gano ta atomatik ta hanyar dogarorin fakiti da tsarukan da ake sa ran samu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Adadin kwanakin da wuraren dubawa, fayilolin shigarwa/fitarwa/kuskure da aka yi nuni da su, da kuma layin aikin Batch API da ya ƙare (an kammala/ya gaza/an soke/lokaci ya ƙare) za su kasance kafin share su ta aikin tsaftacewa ta atomatik. Yana fara aiki ne kawai bayan an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ya yi daidai da lokacin riƙe sakamakon Batch API na OpenAI. Ba ya shafar hanyar `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` da mai gudanarwa ya kunna, wadda bisa tsari take ci gaba da aiki ba tare da wani sharadin shekaru ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Idan `true`, ba da damar aikin tsaftacewa ta atomatik ya share ayyukan Batch API da suka kammala (da wuraren ajiyarsu) bayan `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, sannan ya goge abun cikin BLOB na fayilolin da aka ɗora bayan nasu `expires_at`. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: kowace shigarwa da ke akwai za ta ci gaba da riƙe wannan bayanin daidai yadda take a baya har sai mai gudanarwa ya kunna shi. Haka kuma tutar fasali ce da za a iya gyarawa daga dashboard — duba `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Lokacin aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken lafiyar DB gaba ɗaya yayin farawa. Yana da amfani ga ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci da gwaje-gwajen haɗin kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta kunna madaukin binciken lafiyar DB, ko da kuwa a al'ada za a tsallake shi (misali, ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake shirya native-runtime yayin `npm install`. Yana da amfani a shigarwar CI/headless inda an riga an gina sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Sauya kundin adireshin da mai gudanar da ƙaura yake dubawa. Yana da amfani lokacin aika ƙaurorin da aka haɗa cikin gini na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundin adireshin ƙaura a matsayin shigarwar `namespace=dir` waɗanda alamar raba hanyar dandali ta raba (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Ana rubuta fayilolin da aka samu a wurin a matsayin `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarrabawar da ke ɗauke da nata ƙaurorin ba za ta taɓa cin karo da guraben lambobin upstream ba. Shiga mara inganci, namespace mara inganci, ko kundin adireshi da babu shi zai haifar da kuskure yayin farawa maimakon tsallake schema ba tare da sanarwa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Iyakar kariya ta yawan ƙaurorin da ke jira (#3416). Farawa zai tsaya idan ƙaurorin da ke jira a kan DB da ke akwai sun fi wannan adadin (yana kariya daga teburin bin diddigi da aka goge). Ƙara shi don dawo da tsohon madadin bayanai; saita zuwa `0` don kashe binciken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Kundin aikin ƙofar sakin `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan 12 GB (bishiyoyin shigarwa biyu na ~3 GB tare da tarball), don haka kada ya gudana a kan ƙaramin tmpfs — a kan self-hosted runner ɗin, `/tmp` tmpfs ne mai 12 GB da ke amfani da RAM kuma ƙofar ta cika shi, lamarin da ya yanke kunshin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(tsohon ƙima a cikin lamba)_ | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tazarar flush (ms) ga marubucin kashe kuɗi/farashi na rukuni. Ƙananan ƙimomi suna rage haɗa rubuce-rubuce; manyan ƙimomi suna rage gogayya a DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(tsohon ƙima a cikin lamba)_ | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar kashe kuɗi da za a riƙe a buffer kafin tilasta flush. Ƙara shi a turawar da ke da babban QPS; rage shi idan iyakantaccen amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa ya fi muhimmanci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` don fitar da rajistan debug na `[ProxyFetch]` a hanyar relay ta Vercel. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa don kauce wa fallasa alamun hanyar zirga-zirga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Cikakkiyar hanyar binary na proxy core na gida (fayil mai suna `sing-box`) wanda ke bincika tsarin core da aka samar kafin ya maye gurbin fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Idan ba a saita ba, yana rubuta `<path>.generated` kawai kusa da fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Muhallin host kawai: ba a taɓa adana shi a ma'ajiyar bayanai ko karɓarsa ta management API, saboda ana aiwatar da binary ɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Yadda proxy core na gida yake sake lodawa bayan an maye gurbin config da aka tabbatar: jerin argument na JSON da ake gudanarwa ba tare da shell ba, ko kuma daidai `external` lokacin da mai sa ido na waje yake amfani da fayil ɗin. Ba a taɓa adana shi a ma'ajiyar bayanai ko fallasa shi ta API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don haɗa IP na client/egress da prefix na account a cikin cikakken layin rajistan process na `[ProxyEgress]`. Ana barinsa a KASHE ta tsohuwa don kada rajistan process ya fallasa IP ko prefix na account.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan tantance lokacin kowace buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin ƙimar amfani na CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(ta atomatik)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Takamaiman path da binciken lafiyar container zai gwada. Idan ba a saita ba, binciken zai samo shi daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita shi yana sake kunna endpoint na sa ido mai zurfi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima marar fanko don fitar da bayanan tantance `[omniroute completion]` daga paths na cache na kammalawar shell na CLI (karantawa/sabuntawa/rubutawa). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — waɗannan caches suna gazawa ba tare da sanarwa ba domin cache da ya ɓace ko ya lalace kada ya taɓa karya kammalawa ta tab.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin tsawon lokacin sake gwadawa ga kowane abu na batch (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan lokaci ana yi musu alamar sun gaza.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkiri na asali (ms) don exponential backoff yayin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin iyaka (ms) ga exponential backoff tsakanin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin batches da za a sarrafa a lokaci guda. Ƙara shi don ƙara yawan aiki; bar shi ƙasa don kauce wa cunkoson iyakar ƙimar buƙatu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin canza persistent database da yake akwai, migration runner yana wallafa cikakken,
> content-addressed snapshot a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Wallafawa tana buƙatar filesystem
> da ke goyon bayan hard links na cikin filesystem ɗaya, marasa overwrite, tare da durable file sync. POSIX hosts kuma
> suna buƙatar directory sync; a Windows, Node na iya ƙin directory handles, don haka OmniRoute yana flush na
> fayil ɗin da aka wallafa kuma yana ɗaukar directory-entry sync a matsayin iya ƙoƙari kawai.
> Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka mount ba zai iya samar da waɗannan tabbacin ba, farawa zai gaza cikin aminci kafin aiwatar da
> migration. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa volume mai waɗannan primitives; kada a yi amfani da
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don tsallake amincin migration.

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                  | Tsarawa                                                                             |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ci gaban gida**       | Bar duk tsoffin saituna. DB yana a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                     |
| **Docker**              | `DATA_DIR=/data` + mount volume a `/data`.                                          |
| **An ɓoye yayin ajiya** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + adana backups na key ɗin! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Gwaji**            | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — na wucin gadi ne, ba ya buƙatar ɓoyewa.            |

---

## 3. Cibiyar Sadarwa & Tashoshin Sadarwa

| Sauyi                                       | Tsoho                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar sadarwa don duka Dashboard UI da wuraren ƙarshen API (yanayin tashar sadarwa guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                   | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ƙaramin hanyar URL don gabatar da OmniRoute a bayan reverse proxy (yana saita `basePath` na Next.js; karkatarwar tabbatar da asali tana la'akari da basePath). Misali `/omniroute`. A cikin Docker ana haɗa ƙimar yayin `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); hotunan tushe da aka riga aka gina za su iya amfani da wata ƙimar lokacin aiki sau ɗaya a farkon container kafin Next.js ya fara. Saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa asalin jama'a wanda ya haɗa da wannan ƙaramar hanyar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                   | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Kwafin `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` da burauza ke iya gani, wanda ake saka kai tsaye a lokacin ginawa domin nunin wurin ƙarshen dashboard ya nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` idan ba a saita shi ba. Sake ginawa bayan canza shi (`basePath` na Next ana ƙayyade shi ne lokacin ginawa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ba a saita ba = ba za a taɓa saka shi cikin frame ba)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Ba da damar saka shafukan HTML cikin iframe bisa zaɓi. Idan ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana zuwa da `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita zuwa `vscode` domin gabatar da shafukan (dashboard, shiga, takardu, shafin sauka) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma ba tare da `X-Frame-Options` ba, ta yadda VS Code Simple Browser zai iya nuna su (yanayin `dashboardOpen: "editor"` na OmniCopilot). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, sunayen madadin matakin tushe) tana ci gaba da amfani da tsauraran headers a kowane hali. `vscode` kaɗai ake ganewa — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Na lokacin ginawa ne: sake ginawa bayan canza shi (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don hotuna; saita shi a kan shigarwar da aka riga aka gina ba zai yi tasiri ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana gabatar da proxy API na `/v1/*` a wannan tashar sadarwa ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                                | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adireshin da tashar sadarwar API za ta ɗaure da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana gabatar da Dashboard UI a wannan tashar sadarwa ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(an kashe)_                                         | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Yana iyakance haɗin tattaunawa masu gudana a lokaci guda; buƙatun da suka zarce iyakar suna samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau tana kunna kariyar; rashin saita shi ko `0` yana kashe ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Tsayayyen ID na musamman na wannan gateway lokacin haɗa instances na OmniRoute a jere. Yana ba da damar binciken madauwari na peer mai shigowa. Haruffan da aka yarda da su: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; matsakaicin haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | Tushen URLs na OmniRoute da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za su iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. URLs na upstream da aka amince da su a sarari ne kawai za su karɓi metadata na peer; duk sauran masu samarwa ba a taɓa su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                                      | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Matsakaicin adadin instans na OmniRoute da aka ziyarta a baya waɗanda za a karɓa a kan buƙata mai sarƙaƙƙiya (`1`-`32`). Instans da aka maimaita ko kasafin tsallake-tsallake da ya ƙare zai dawo da HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Yana da fifiko a kan `PORT` lokacin da ake aiki a cikin Electron ko wasu wrappers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                                  | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port don sabar sa ido kai tsaye ta WebSocket a ainihin lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adireshin bind don sabar WebSocket kai tsaye. An riga an ba da izini ga origins na dashboard na loopback ta tsohuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Ƙarin sunayen host da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka yarda da su don origins na WebSocket kai tsaye. Sabanin `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakkun URLs na origin), yana daidaita ɓangaren host kawai — yana da amfani ga saitunan LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL na jama'a don WebSocket na dashboard kai tsaye (a ɓangaren burauza). Saita shi lokacin da aka sa reverse proxy ko Cloudflare Tunnel a gaban sabar WS (misali `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); burauzar za ta haɗu a can maimakon `ws://hostname:20132`. Haka kuma ana amfani da ɓangaren pathname a matsayin hanyar haɓakawa ta WebSocket (tsoho: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
