| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                                   | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` da `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don kashe sabar WebSocket ta ainihin lokaci (ana kunna ta ta tsohuwa, kuma an ɗaure ta da loopback). Maɓallin CI/harness ne da ke kashe keɓantaccen helper script na WebSocket kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Iyakar ƙimar relay ga kowane (token, IP), buƙatu/minti. A cikin ƙwaƙwalwa, ga kowane instans. `0` ko ƙasa da sifili yana kashe ƙofar ma'aunin IP (iyakar DB ta kowane token har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                             | Babban tsarin Next.js                                                    | Yana sarrafa yawan bayanan log, caching, bayyana cikakkun bayanan kuskure, da ingantawar Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — tsohon ƙimar lamba)                     | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack shi ne bundler na asali don `npm run dev` da `npm run build` (ginawa mai saurin sau 2-3, bisa gwajin benchmark). Saita shi zuwa `0` don komawa webpack a Windows, lokacin da aka ci karo da rashin dacewar native binding / bundler, **ko a kwamfutoci masu ƙarancin RAM** — an san cewa ginawar Turbopack ta production a wannan jerin sigar Next.js (16.2.x) tana kaiwa matsanancin amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa fiye da webpack a manyan jadawalin modules (gyaran fitar da ƙwaƙwalwar Turbopack na Next 16.3 bai zama stable ba tukuna); komawa webpack yana amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa mafi ƙasa sosai. Duba #6409. **Ginawar hoton Docker tana amfani da `0` (webpack) a matsayin tsohon ƙima**: `Dockerfile` yana ayyana `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` domin `docker build .` kai tsaye (Railway da sauran masu masaukin dannawa sau ɗaya, da masu ɗaukar nauyin kansu na wucin gadi) kada OOM ya kashe shi a kan builder mai iyakantacciyar ƙwaƙwalwa; miƙa `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` don gina hoton da Turbopack. Duba `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` → Albarkatun lokacin ginawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken lafiyar mutuncin SQLite yayin farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin farawa a manyan rumbunan bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | systemd (ka'idar sd_notify)                                              | systemd ne ke saita shi lokacin da process ke gudana ƙarƙashin service unit mai haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute yana karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Kada mai amfani ya taɓa saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                        | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ko da ana gudana ƙarƙashin systemd unit. Notifier ba ya yin komai a wajen systemd ko da kuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                                                | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazarar lokaci (ms) don scheduler na binciken lafiyar credential da ke gudana a bango. Mafi ƙaranci: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                                 | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) na halin lafiyar credential da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                                  | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin haɗin provider na lokaci-lokaci da ke gudana a bango. Ana cire search providers (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a cikin `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe daga binciken — "validation" ɗinsu ainihin upstream query ce mai kuɗi, don haka ba a taɓa bincika lafiyarsu ta timer (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                                      | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Saita zuwa `1` don bai wa authenticated caller damar neman `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, wanda ke ɗaukar samfurin completions surface sau ɗaya a kowane TTL. Anonymous callers ba sa taɓa kunna probe ɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                                | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Bind address na Next.js dev/start server. Yana maye gurbin tsohon ƙimar `0.0.0.0` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Bind address da Playwright runner ke amfani da shi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Tsohon ƙimarsa ita ce `127.0.0.1` don gwaje-gwajen hermetic. **Kada a yi amfani da shi don `omniroute serve`** — yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` maimakon haka (POSIX shells suna saita `HOSTNAME` ta atomatik zuwa sunan na'ura; `.env` ba zai iya maye gurbinsa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                                | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adireshin ɗaurewa don `omniroute serve`. Yana kauce wa karo da canjin POSIX shell na `HOSTNAME` (wanda bash/zsh ke saita shi koyaushe zuwa sunan na’urar). Idan ba a saita shi ba, zai koma `0.0.0.0`. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Yanayin Tashoshin Sadarwa

```
┌─────────────────────────── Tasha Guda (tsoho) ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Allon Kulawa: http://localhost:20128                                     │
│  → API:          http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                 │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Rarrababbun Tashoshi ────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Allon Kulawa: http://localhost:20128                                     │
│  → API:          http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                  │
│  Amfani: Bayyana API ga LAN yayin taƙaita Allon Kulawa zuwa localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker na Samarwa ───────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Yana danganta tashoshin container zuwa tashoshin host a docker-compose.prod.yml. │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tantancewa

| Sauyi                                       | Tsoho                                                                                                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Salt da ake haɗawa da masu gano kayan masarufi domin samar da sawun yatsan na’ura. Canza shi a kowace turawa domin warewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ba a saita ba = salt na bazuwar kowace girkawa da ake adanawa a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Salt na HMAC don samar da token ɗin tantancewa na CLI na cikin gida. Saita wannan ƙimar yana sabunta duk token ɗin CLI a kan na’urar kuma koyaushe yana da fifiko kan salt ɗin da aka adana. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                              | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana saita tutar `Secure` a kan kukis ɗin zama. **Dole ne ya zama `true`** yayin aiki a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                              | Tsakiyar manhajar API                                    | Idan `true`, dole ne duk buƙatun proxy na `/v1/*` su ƙunshi ingantacciyar maɓallin API. **Wannan tutar ba ta taƙaita `GET /v1/models` ba**, wanda a maimakon haka yana bin tsarin shiga na dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) — don haka samun `401` daga `/v1/models` BA ya nufin an kare inference. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                              | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar bayyana cikakkun ƙimomin maɓallin API a cikin UI na Dashboard. Ana iya saita shi daga Tutocin Fasali na Dashboard; yana da haɗarin tsaro a kan instances da ake rabawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(fanko)_                                                                                            | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID na maɓallan API da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ba a rubuta buƙatunsu a log ba (bin ƙa’idar GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(ba a saita ba = mara iyaka)_                                                                       | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin buƙatu na kowace rana da ake amfani da shi a matsayin madadin ga maɓallan API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ɗinsu null ne. Idan ba a saita ba ko fanko ne: babu iyaka ta ɓoye (#2289, #11017). `0` ma haka yake (mara iyaka). Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau N tana kunna N/rana, 5N/mako, 20N/wata. Ƙimomin da ba fanko ba amma tsarin su ba daidai ba ne suna komawa ga tsoffin iyakokin 1000/rana, 5000/mako, 20000/wata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Matsakaicin girman jikin buƙata da aka yarda da shi. Yana ƙin payloads da suka wuce wannan iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ainihin jikin buƙata da ya kai ko ya wuce wannan mataki yana karɓar heavyweight admission lease na atomic na cikin process kafin a yi parsing na JSON (hanyar BYTE, ciki har da `POST /v1/responses`). Yana da irin hanyar kuɓuta ta healthy-headroom ta [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) kamar structure-heavy; har yanzu `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) suna iyakance shi don kada [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ya dawo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri na hanyar chat da ake tilastawa bisa bytes da aka karanta yayin shigar da bayanai mai iyaka, har da buƙatun da ba su da `Content-Length`, ko suke da marar inganci, ko na ƙarya; abin da ya wuce iyaka yana karɓar `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(ba a saita ba — babu iyakar adadin buƙatu)_                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** wannan tsohuwar iyakar ADADIN buƙatu yanzu tana aiki ne kawai idan an saita ta kai tsaye. Idan aka bar ta ba a saita ba (abin da yake tsoho), karɓar manyan buƙatun chat maimakon haka ana sarrafa ta da `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — kasafin BYTES da ake ƙirƙira ta atomatik bisa ainihin iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar aikin a cikin **process guda ɗaya** (heap na V8 guda ɗaya). Buƙatun `/v1/responses` guda biyu masu cin karo da juna na kusan tokens 750k suna durƙusar da heaps na kusan 12 GiB (#7849) — wannan gargaɗin **kasafin ƙwaƙwalwa** ne, ba matsakaicin iyakar samfur mai tsauri na 2 ba. Process mai lafiya (heap da ke ƙasa da shed ratio) ZAI IYA karɓar ƙarin dogayen `/v1/responses` a lokaci guda ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Dubban goma na dogayen abokan cinikin SSE (40–50) batu ne na heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ba “iyakar 2” ba. Ƙara wannan a makance domin “amfani da host” yana sake haddasa #7849. Ninka **heaps** ta amfani da **`DATA_DIR`s`masu zaman kansu guda N** (#11024); kada a taɓa amfani da`replicas>1` a kan fayil ɗin SQLite guda ɗaya.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(ana ƙirƙira ta atomatik)_                                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** saitin maye gurbin kasafin bytes na shigarwa da ake ƙirƙira ta atomatik (25% na mafi ƙanƙantar iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar V8/cgroup da aka raba da ninkawar wucin gadi ta 8x). Ƙimomin da aka ƙirƙira da waɗanda aka saita kai tsaye ana taƙaita su zuwa 8 MiB–2 GiB. Body da ya fi kasafin da ke aiki girma yana gaza nan take da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; cunkoso tsakanin bodies waɗanda kowannensu za a iya sarrafawa ya ci gaba da kasancewa wanda za a iya sake gwadawa da `503`. Abokan cinikin SSE masu dogon haɗi guda 40–50 batu ne na wannan kasafi + heap, ba “iyakar 2” mai tsauri ba. Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin daidaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                               | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Shed ratio na matsin heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don karɓar manyan buƙatun BYTE da STRUCTURE (#10183, #10268, #10437). Babbar buƙata mai gudana tare da wasu bayan `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` ana kore ta da `503` mai yiwuwar sake gwadawa ne kawai idan heap SHIMA ya kai ko ya wuce wannan ratio; idan heap ɗin yana lafiya, ana karɓar ta ta healthy-headroom maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (tsoho `1`)                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarin ƙarfin da aka iyakance don hanyar sauri ta healthy-heap (#10437) a kan **duka** STRUCTURE da BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, har da bodies ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan iyaka ba, kowace buƙata yayin aiki mai yawa amma heap na lafiya tana tsallake tsarin karɓa ba tare da wata iyaka ba. Da zarar leases masu gudana tare sun kai wannan adadi ta hanyar healthy-heap, sauran buƙatun lokacin aiki mai yawa suna komawa ga HANYAR jira mai iyaka/korewa ƊAYA da ake amfani da ita ƙarƙashin ainihin matsin heap. `0` yana kashe hanyar tsallakewar gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin saƙonnin da ke ware buƙatar chat a matsayin babba ko da body ɗinta yana ƙasa da iyakar bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin tools da ke ware buƙatar chat a matsayin babba ko da body ɗinta yana ƙasa da iyakar bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                              | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙiyasin tokens mai taka-tsantsan bisa girman string wanda ke ware buƙata a matsayin babba; wannan wakilin kuɗin karɓa ne, ba tsarin provider na lissafin tokens don cajin kuɗi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (a kashe)                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyakar tarihin chat ta zaɓi. An kashe ta a tsoho: adadin saƙonni manufofin deployment ne, ba siffar bai ɗaya ta kowace buƙata ba, kuma sanya iyaka a nan yana ƙin tattaunawa da `413` na ƙarshe kafin pipeline na compression ya iya sa a sarrafa su. Girman heap yana da iyaka ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da korar da matsin heap ke jawowa. Saita ƙima mai kyau a deployments masu ƙarancin ƙwaƙwalwa waɗanda ke buƙatar iyaka mai tsauri; abin da ya wuce iyaka daga nan yana karɓar tsararren `413` mai nuna ana buƙatar compression.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                   | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri ga response na upstream mara streaming da ake buffer gaba ɗaya cikin ƙwaƙwalwa. Bayan wannan ana soke upstream reader kuma buƙatar tana gaza da sauri maimakon ci gaba da ƙara string marar iyaka har sai heap ya ƙare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Matsakaicin adadin bytes na waya da ake turawa daga headers na amsar upstream. Idan an wuce kasafin, ana cire headers masu ƙarancin fifiko (misali, na musamman `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) domin a ci gaba da kasancewa cikin iyakokin headers na reverse-proxy da aka saba amfani da su. Saita daraja mafi girma don tura ƙarin metadata na upstream, amma hakan zai ƙara girman header na amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                    | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Tsohon jerin izinin CORS na origin guda ɗaya. Fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` don sabbin turawa. CORS na abokan cinikin API na burauza masu asali daban kawai ne; rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tabbatar da sahihancinsu suna amfani da buƙatun asali ɗaya tare da kariyar CSRF mai alaƙa da zaman maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                    | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin izinin CORS da waƙafi ya raba. Ba a aika wildcard sai idan an saita `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` a bayyane.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                              | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Hanyar kaucewa ta ci gaba kawai don maimaita duk wani `Origin` na burauza. Kada a kunna ta a turawa na gama-gari ko na samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                               | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Tsohon suna na madadin. Ana karanta maɓallin dashboard ɗinsa (override na DB) kafin wannan darajar muhalli; `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` a kowane ɗayansu yana kashe binciken host kamar yadda `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` ke yi, ko da an ajiye maɓallin dashboard na wannan flag a kashe; sauran darajoji suna barin kariyar ga flags biyun da ke ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                              | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana kashe binciken host na kariyar URL mai fita, har da toshe cloud-metadata (duk 169.254.0.0/16, metadata.google.internal), a hanyoyin provider da ke bin yanayin kariyar: tabbatar da URL, gano model, base URLs na provider-node (har da nodes na rerank na nesa), da gwajin proxy-fallback, tare da sauran hanyoyin fita masu alaƙa da provider kamar ɗauko hoto/media daga nesa. Haka kuma yana ba da damar targets na webhook masu zaman kansu, ban da endpoints na cloud-metadata/link-local, waɗanda za su ci gaba da kasancewa a toshe ko da an kunna wannan flag. Buƙatun chat da aka aika ta `BaseExecutor.execute()` suna ci gaba da binciken cloud-metadata nasu. Providers na gida suna aiki da saitunan tsohuwa: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (tsoho `true`) ya riga ya ba da damar URLs na gida da LAN a hanyoyin tabbatarwa, ganowa, da provider-node; gwajin proxy-fallback da targets na webhook masu zaman kansu suna duba wannan flag kawai kuma suna toshe hosts na gida/LAN yayin da yake kashe. Yana rinjayar `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Darajar da aka ajiye daga maɓallin dashboard tana da fifiko a kan wannan variable. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                               | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Bada damar URLs na provider a adiresoshin gida/masu zaman kansu (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, private ranges) don tabbatar da provider, gano model, chat, da sauran hanyoyin fita masu alaƙa da provider waɗanda yanayin kariyar ke sarrafawa. **Tsoho `true`** (gida-farko): sannan kariyar tana toshe endpoints na cloud-metadata — duk 169.254.0.0/16 tare da sanannun hostnames na metadata (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Saita `false` don yanayin jama'a-kawai, wanda kuma yake toshe hosts masu zaman kansu da loopback. Kariyar tana bincika hostname ko IP literal yadda aka rubuta shi, kafin duk wani binciken DNS; a yanayin jama'a-kawai safe-fetch wrapper yana kuma resolve hostname ɗin sannan ya ƙi duk wata amsar mai zaman kanta. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yana kashe waɗannan binciken a hanyoyin da layinsa ya jera. Providers na gida da aka gina a ciki, kamar LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, da Triton, suna tsallake duk binciken host don tabbatar da key da chat. Darajar da aka ajiye daga maɓallin dashboard tana da fifiko a kan wannan variable. (#5066)           |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                              | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Bada damar routes na `/v1/audio/*` (rubuta sauti zuwa rubutu, magana, fassarori) su yi amfani da provider node mai jituwa da OpenAI wanda aka hosting a wajen localhost. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura audio zuwa host na nesa yana canza asalin egress kuma dole ya zama shawarar operator da aka yi a bayyane. Ana ba da damar nodes na loopback/masu zaman kansu (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) koyaushe kuma wannan ba ya shafarsu. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                              | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Bada damar `POST /v1/rerank` (da matakin rerank na loopback na memory engine) ya yi amfani da provider node mai jituwa da OpenAI wanda aka hosting a wajen localhost — na'urar LAN ko Tailscale peer da ke tafiyar da TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da sauransu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura hanya zuwa host na nesa yana canza asalin egress kuma dole ya zama shawarar operator da aka yi a bayyane. Ana ba da damar nodes na loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) koyaushe kuma wannan ba ya shafarsu. Base URL na node na nesa dole ne kuma ya wuce manufar URL mai fita ta provider (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(babu komai)_                                                                                       | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Sunayen runduna (waɗanda aka raba da wakafi ko farin sarari) waɗanda tsarin tura buƙatu na provider-node yake ɗauka a matsayin na gida — galibi sunan sabis na Docker/Compose na reranker, TEI ko audio sidecar a kan hanyar sadarwar Docker ta OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), don haka node ɗin ba ya buƙatar IP na container da aka ƙayyade kai tsaye. Rundunonin da aka jera sun cancanci `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` da binciken lafiyar tsarin na gida ba tare da kunna `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` ga kowane node na nesa ba, kuma ana kiransu kai tsaye — ba a taɓa bi ta `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ko pinned proxy na wata haɗi ba. Har yanzu suna aika API key na haɗin. Sunaye masu lakabi guda ɗaya da sunayen `.internal`/`.local` ne kawai ake karɓa; ana yin watsi da public FQDNs, IP literals da sunayen cloud-metadata tare da gargaɗi. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                              | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, a kashe shiga da kalmar sirri domin masu amfani su iya tantance kansu ta hanyar OIDC Single Sign-On kawai. Haka kuma ana karɓar bare alias `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Dashboard Feature Flag mai wannan key ɗin ne yake da fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                              | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Bare alias na `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Jerin Dubawa na Ƙarfafa Tsaro

```bash
# Mafi ƙarancin tsaron tsarin samarwa:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Yana buƙatar HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Tantance duk kiran proxy
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Kada a taɓa bayyana keys a UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Iyakar 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Tsabtace Bayanai Masu Shigowa & Kare PII

OmniRoute yana samar da kariya mai matakai biyu: binciken yunƙurin kutse a ɓangaren buƙata da cire PII a ɓangaren martani.

> **⚠️ Iyakoki:** Waɗannan matakan kariya bincike ne na _hasashe gwargwadon ƙoƙari_, ba cikakken bangon kariya daga kutse ta hanyar prompt ko tsarin PII DLP ba. Za su iya haifar da sakamako na ƙarya (kamar yi wa prompts na persona/RPG marasa lahani alama) da kuma kasa gano barazana (kamar leetspeak, tazara, da tsarukan da ba na Turanci ba). Su kaɗai ba su isa wajen bin ƙa'idodi ba. Daidaita modes ɗin kuma gwada su da zirga-zirgarku kafin ku dogara da su.

### Ɓangaren Buƙata: Kariyar Kutse ta Prompt

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna binciken saƙonni masu shigowa domin gano tsarukan kutse ta prompt.                                                                                                                           |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufofin kutse: `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = ƙin buƙatar tare da 400. Tsohon `redact` **ba ya** cire rubutun kutse; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` domin sake rubuta PII na buƙata. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                  |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin matakin tsanani da `MODE=block` zai ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Ana sa ido kawai kan tsarukan matsakaicin tsanani sai an rage matakin.                                   |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                       |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Idan `true`, ɓoye PII a cikin buƙatu masu shigowa (ba tare da dogaro da yanayin kutse ba).                                                                                                         |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Ɓoye sanannun tsarukan API-key / secret-token daga bayanan buƙata/martani. Sai an zaɓa a kunna; yana kwaikwayon `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                           |

### Ɓangaren Martani: Mai Tsabtace PII

| Maɓalli                          | Tsoho    | Fayil ɗin Tushe           | Bayani                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika martanin LLM domin gano PII da ya fallasa kafin mayar da shi ga abokin ciniki. |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = ɓoye PII, `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = watsar da dukkan martanin.   |

### Mai Tsabtace Context na Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`   | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yana cire context na active-editor da aka haɗa kai tsaye (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` kuma yana ɓoye abubuwan da ke cikin fayiloli masu muhimmanci da aka haɗa a bayyane. Amintacce ta tsoho; saita zuwa `0` domin kashewa. |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                    | Saituna                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Bin ƙa'idodin kamfani** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (toshe kutse + ɓoye PII na buƙata/martani; modes ɗin ba su dogara da juna ba) |
| **Sa ido kawai**          | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — yana rubuta logs amma ba ya taɓa toshewa                                                                                                     |
| **Amfani na kashin kai**  | Bar duk a kashe — babu ƙarin nauyi                                                                                                                                                                         |

---

## 6. Manufofin Kayan Aiki & Daidaita Hanya

| Sauyi                                                       | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                       | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                        | Yana sarrafa damar LLM ta amfani da kayan aiki/kiran ayyuka. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka jera kawai, `denylist` = duk banda waɗanda aka jera, `disabled` = babu ƙuntatawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`     | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`            | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin JSON na ƙa'idojin sarrafa payload (gyare-gyaren upstream na kowane model/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                           | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`            | Tazarar sake lodawa (ms) don sake loda fayil ɗin ƙa'idojin payload kai tsaye. Mafi ƙaranci `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                          | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                   | Zaɓi na son rai: aika ID ɗin model `claude-*` marasa prefix daga abokan hulɗar Claude Code ta asusun OAuth na Claude Code maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai samarwa. Bayyanannun prefix na mai samarwa har yanzu suna da fifiko. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ta maɓallin kunnawa a dashboard na shafin mai samar da Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                              | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`             | Iyakar gudanarwa a lokaci guda ga kowane model don haɗin round-robin (#9100). An kafa ƙayyadadden iyakar semaphore na haɗin round-robin zuwa buƙatu 3 masu gudana lokaci guda ga kowane model ba tare da damar sauyawa ba, wanda ke jera zirga-zirgar da ke da yawan gudanarwa a lokaci guda a bayan wannan iyaka. Ana tabbatar da cewa yana da `>= 1`, sannan a taƙaita shi zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                          | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                | Zaɓi mai haɗari wanda ke tsallake binciken taga-context / iyakar token na shigarwa na OmniRoute don buƙatun model guda ɗaya kai tsaye. Masu samar da upstream har yanzu suna tilasta ainihin iyakokinsu; matse prompt da iyakar token na fitarwa ta model ɗin kansa suna ci gaba da aiki. Tsarin fifikon da ake amfani da shi shi ne maye gurbin Feature Flags DB > environment variable > tsoho; ba a buƙatar sake farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`         | Takardar YAML `providers:` ta cikin layi (RIC-738, D4). Idan an saita (tare da ko ba tare da block na `strategy:` ba), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkata zuwa mashigar haɗaɗɗiyar self-hosted mai dacewa da OpenAI maimakon cloud pipeline. Idan ba a saita ba (tsoho): hanyar tana wucewa kai tsaye zuwa cloud pipeline da ake da shi. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`         | Hanya zuwa fayil ɗin YAML mai ɗauke da takardar `providers:` iri ɗaya da `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don turawa da suka fi son fayil maimakon environment variable ta cikin layi. Kowanne daga cikinsu yana kunna mashigar self-hosted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba — hanya a buɗe)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`         | Maɓallin API na bai ɗaya na zaɓi don haɗaɗɗiyar mashigar self-hosted (tsarin farko na D5, an keɓe shi don tsarin quota na kowane maɓalli). Idan an saita, dole ne buƙatu su ƙunshi `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Idan ba a saita ba: hanyar a buɗe take, daidai da tsarin local-provider na self-hosted da ake da shi (turawa ta loopback/cibiyar sadarwa amintacciya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`       | Takardar YAML `strategy:` ta cikin layi don injin daidaita hanya mai tabbataccen sakamako (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, mai katse cooldown, fifikon farashi, mai la'akari da latency, jerin fallback. Yana maye gurbin block na `strategy:` ta cikin layi da aka saka a cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, ga kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
