| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`       | Hanya zuwa fayil ɗin YAML mai ɗauke da takardar `strategy:` iri ɗaya da `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don turawa da suka fi son fayil maimakon environment variable ta cikin layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`     | Saita `1` don dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamakon da ba a sa ido a kansa ba kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya tantance tarihin saƙonni, kuma wannan ya shafi ID na zaman da abokin ciniki ya bayar ma. Gudanar da zaman turawa bai canza ba, kuma ba a share bayanan da suke akwai. Ya dace da tsarin da ba ya amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma yake son teburin zagayen tattaunawa ya daina ƙaruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts` | Maɓallin API don dabarar na'ura mai zaɓar hanya ta auto-combo ta `nadir`, wadda ake amfani da ita idan ba a saita `config.nadir.apiKey` na combo ɗin ba. Na zaɓi ne: kira marasa maɓalli suna shiga matakin masu amfani ba tare da suna ba na Nadir, wanda ke da iyakacin adadin kira bisa kowane IP, don haka ba zai yi amfani a bayan wata ƙofa da ake rabawa ba. Bayani ne mai muhimmanci; fi son filin kowane combo idan combo-combo suna amfani da asusu daban-daban.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`       | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts` | Asalin URL na API na yanke shawara na Nadir, wanda ake amfani da shi idan ba a saita `config.nadir.baseUrl` na combo ɗin ba. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don Nadir da aka karɓi bakuncinsa da kai, wanda ke riƙe farkon prompt a cikin hanyar sadarwarka. Ana amincewa da `/v1` a ƙarshen URL kuma a cire shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                       | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`           | Tagar mako-mako don iyakokin amfanin USD na kowane maɓallin API (tilasta buƙata, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: makon yana ƙarewa a lokacin sake saita iyakar mako-mako mafi kusa daga cikin haɗin upstream da maɓallin zai iya isa gare su, ko kuma ya zama kwanaki 7 masu zagayawa idan babu wanda aka sani. `calendar`: makon gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin ta gaba 00:00, iri ɗaya ga kowane maɓalli. Ana ba da shawarar `calendar` idan ana raba rukunin asusun upstream guda ɗaya tsakanin maɓallai da yawa waɗanda kowannensu ke da kasafin kuɗinsa. Ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba suna komawa zuwa `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(yankin lokaci na process)_     | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`           | Yankin lokaci na IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda ke ayyana Litinin 00:00 ga tagar mako-mako ta `calendar`. Idan ba a saita ba ko bai inganta ba: za a yi amfani da yankin lokaci na process na OmniRoute (`TZ` na host ko container, wanda sau da yawa ba a saita shi ba, wato Lokacin Duniya Mai Daidaituwa, a cikin hotunan container). Ana yin watsi da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin kayan aiki da aka yarda da su a cikin buƙata ta tsohuwa (tsohon ƙimar OpenAI). Cikakkun lambobi masu kyau kawai; idan ba a saita ba ko ƙimar ba ta inganta ba, za a ci gaba da amfani da `128`. Mai bayarwa da ke da sananniyar iyaka ko iyakar da aka sa tun da wuri (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) har yanzu yana amfani da iyakarsa — wannan yana sauya rufin tsohuwar ƙima ne kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(a kunne)_                      | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`              | Kiran kayan aiki ta kwaikwayon prompt na SYNTX.ai: idan ba a saita ba ko kuma an saita `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, ana shigar da kundin kayan aiki na gida cikin prompt; kowace ƙima dabam (misali `0`) tana tsallake wannan shigarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Sirri ne; fi son filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Sirri ne; fi son filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint na iyakokin amfani na CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). A sauya shi don relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL ɗinka na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan callbacks na OAuth ko hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka samar dole ne su yi amfani da wannan hostname. Idan babu wannan, callbacks na OAuth na iya kasa saboda redirect_uri ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka samar na iya nuni zuwa asalin container na ciki.
>
> Bar `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na loopback/container na ciki don ayyukan server-to-server. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na browser ko hostname na jama'a don internal self-fetches masu ɗauke da credentials.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tabbatar da izini ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen public base URL: dashboard yana aika same-origin unsafe requests tare da CSRF token da ke daure da session. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana tattara public-origin validation wuri guda don haɗin browser da ba na dashboard ba: ana fara amincewa da explicit public URL env vars; ana watsi da raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` headers sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa immediate proxy peer alamar token a matsayin amintacce. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara same-origin dashboard requests; CORS na cross-origin browser clients ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakilin Fita

Tura kiran masu samar da LLM na upstream ta hanyar wakilin HTTP ko SOCKS5 don sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita, zaɓar hanya bisa yanki, ko ɓoye adireshin IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` yana amfani da `true` a matsayin ƙimar asali. Saita shi zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko
`off` don tilasta amfani da HTTP/1.1 a kan masu aikawa na Undici da ke cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: fita kai tsaye, wakilan HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relays, da
sake gwada haɗinsu ta sabon socket. Wannan yana kiyaye tabbatar da takardar shaidar TLS, tantancewar
wakili, iyakokin haɗi, da saitunan keep-alive. Sake kunna OmniRoute bayan
canza shi, domin ana adana masu aikawa a ma'ajiyar wucin gadi kuma ana ƙirƙirar relay agents lokacin farawa.
Wannan sauyin ba ya sarrafa kwaikwayon sawun TLS na wreq ko hanyoyin sufuri na musamman ga masu samarwa.

| Maɓalli                                  | Na asali          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin proxy na SOCKS5 don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sabis na sama. A kashe ta hanyar saita `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Bangaren abokin ciniki                         | Sanin samuwar SOCKS5 a bangaren abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali da aka kunna ta tsohuwa (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saiti daga DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). Tarin proxy da juyawa na kowane asusu za su daina sake ba da wani mamba da ya gaza a yanzu (binciken TCP da aka ƙi, ko kuskuren 429 ta cikinsa) na wani lokaci da ke ninkawa sau biyu duk lokacin da ya sake faruwa, har zuwa iyaka. `false` yana dawo da zaɓi na yau da kullum.                                                                       |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Jerin izini don wuraren da local-core selector-control ke nufi bayan loopback (sunayen host ko IP da waƙafi/sarari ya raba; `*` yana buɗe LAN/na sirri amma ba metadata/link-local ba). Ana yarda da loopback ta http/https ta tsohuwa. Ƙimar DB ta dashboard tana da fifiko kan wannan maɓalli idan an saita ta kuma ba fanko ba ce.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Lokacin tushe (ms) na warewar 429, wanda ke ninkawa sau biyu a kowane maimaituwa. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya yayin farawa. An iyakance shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba za ta koma ga ƙimar asali tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Iyakar (ms) warewar 429. Ana karanta ta sau ɗaya yayin farawa. An iyakance ta zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba za ta koma ga ƙimar asali tare da gargaɗi. Idan iyakar ta yi ƙasa da tushen, za ta koma ga ƙimar asali. Rage iyakar kuma yana taƙaita lokacin manta jerin maimaituwa: ana goge shigarwa bayan `2 x max` daga ƙarewar lokaci.                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali ta zaɓin shiga (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saiti daga DB na dashboard yana da fifiko). Ga masu samarwa waɗanda ake rarraba adadin amfaninsu bisa adireshin fita, a jera memban tarin da ke amfani da adireshin fita iri ɗaya da aka gani na wani memba da aka ƙi kwanan nan a ƙasan membobi masu lafiya kai tsaye. Jeri kawai ake yi, ba a taɓa warewa ba. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke samar da siginar ƙin da yake karantawa. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Tagar rebound (ms) da ke iyakance maimaita faɗakarwar webhook ta `proxy.set_aside` ga kowane memban tarin, domin cunkoson ƙin buƙata ya aika da aukuwa guda ɗaya. Ana karanta ta a kowane fitarwa. An iyakance ta zuwa `1000..3600000`; ƙimar da ta fita daga iyaka ko wadda ba za a iya karantawa ba za ta koma ga ƙimar asali ba tare da gargaɗi ba.                                                                                                                                |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Proxy na HTTP don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sabis na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Proxy na HTTPS don kiraye-kirayen zuwa sabis na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Proxy na bai ɗaya (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Yana ƙayyade maƙasudin echo-IP da gwaje-gwajen fitar proxy ke amfani da shi zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Idan ba a saita ba, gwajin zai fara da `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org`, domin kada a ba da rahoton cewa tunnels masu IPv4-kawai ba sa aiki (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                               | Sunayen host/IP da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a tsallake proxy dominsu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Matsakaicin sockets masu aiki lokaci guda ga kowane HTTP/SOCKS proxy dispatcher da aka adana a cache. Rafukan SSE masu daɗewa kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya idan buƙatu da yawa suna amfani da proxy ɗaya na matakin asusu. Ana iyakance ƙimomin da suka wuce `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Lokacin ƙarewar SOCKS5 handshake (connect) a cikin ms. Ƙara shi idan ana amfani da host guda na residential gateway da cunkoso mai yawa (misali, buƙatu 100 a lokaci guda) — ainihin handshake na iya wuce 10s idan pool ya cika duk da cewa ana iya isa ga proxy, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin kuskuren ƙarya na `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. An iyakance shi zuwa `120000`.                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Idan `false` ne (tsoho), buƙatar da proxy ɗin da aka ware mata ya kasa resolve za a **ƙi karɓa (fail-closed)** maimakon komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye — wannan yana hana fallasa ainihin IP. Saita `true` don dawo da tsohon yanayin komawa ga DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwaikwayi fingerprint na TLS ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana magance toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin providers da aka yarda da su, waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi, don sabon tsarin tura TLS ta proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS yana ci gaba da tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita ba; waɗannan providers kaɗai ne ake turawa ta gadar fingerprint ta Chrome-124. Ana cire Groq (`api.groq.com`) ko da ba a saita jerin izinin ba, saboda Chrome JA3 yana jawo Cloudflare 1010, yayin da native undici ke isa ga API.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Ba mahallin browser na Claude Turnstile Playwright damar yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                                                         | Saitin                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **SOCKS5 ta cikin SSH tunnel**                                 | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                      |
| **HTTP proxy na kamfani**                                      | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`            |
| **Kariya daga fingerprint**                                    | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                                 |
| **Fitar da ake sarrafawa / babu shiga kai tsaye**              | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (tsoho). Buƙatu za su gaza gaba ɗaya idan babu proxy maimakon fallasa bayanai ta hanyar haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / dev — ba da damar komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana dawo da halin kafin ƙarfafa tsaro: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan proxy resolution ya gaza.         |

> **Lura (tsallake NVIDIA validation — #3226):** Endpoint na NVIDIA don tantance API-key
> yana makale idan aka tura shi ta global proxy/TLS-patched fetch (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan yana tsallake
> proxy patch don wannan kiran validation guda ɗaya ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓantaccen yanayi ne da aka rubuta kuma aka iyakance — **ba ya** shafar fitar chat/usage.
> An ɗaure iyakar tsallakewar ta `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗa Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke ganowa da ƙaddamar da sidecars na CLI (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = bincika PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da takamaiman hanyoyi kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano fayilolin binary na CLI (an raba su da alamar wakafi mai digo).                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Sauya kundin gida don karanta saitunan CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya zama cikakkiyar hanya kuma yana cikin gidan tsarin aiki — **ko kuma**, a cikin container, hanyar da aka bind-mount (haka `/host-home` ke aiki). Duk wani abu dabam zai koma kundin gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ba OmniRoute damar rubuta fayilolin saitin CLI (sabunta token, bayanan zama). Saita zuwa `false` domin duk wani yunƙurin rubuta saitin CLI ya gaza tare da takamaiman kuskuren "an kashe rubutawa".                                                                       |

### Daidaita Bayanan Martabar CLI ta Atomatik

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma a kashe suke ta asali. Haka kuma, ana iya kunna ko kashe su daga
dashboard na CLI Code.

| Maɓalli                               | Na Asali | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na `~/.codex/*.config.toml` ta atomatik daga kundin bayanai mai aiki. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Codex mai aiki/na asali, tantancewa, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na Claude Code na `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` ta atomatik daga kundin bayanai mai aiki. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Claude mai aiki/na asali, tantancewa, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Haɗa binary na na'urar masauki cikin container sannan ka gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana fara aiki ne kawai idan an ɗaura hanyar a matsayin bind mount daga
na'urar mai masaukin baki — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba
profile na `host` a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Ana yin watsi da hanyar da ba ta cikin
home na mai amfani da container kuma ba bind mount ba ce, saboda duk abin da aka rubuta a wurin
zai ɓace idan aka sake ƙirƙirar container ɗin.

Image ɗin yana gudana a matsayin `USER node`, don haka `/root` da ba a yi masa mount ba **ba**
ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Variable                                 | Default         | Source File                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(ta atomatik)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta gano container ya kasance a kunne (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatar wannan ne kawai a runtimes da ganowar ta atomatik ba ta gano ba.   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ba da damar rubuta saitunan kayan aikin CLI cikin hanyar container da ba a yi wa mount ba duk da haka. Daidaitaccen zaɓin CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`. |

### Mataimakan Binary na CLI (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan variables suna daidaita halayen binary na CLI na `omniroute` kansa (ba ganowar sidecar
da ke sama ba).

| Variable                              | Default           | Source File                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(system)_        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen output na CLI. Locale na BCP-47 (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana override variables na locale na system (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don tura stdout/stderr na server zuwa terminal a supervised mode. Daidai yake da flag na `--log` a kan `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na tabbatar da na'ura da ake saka a matsayin header na `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana samar da shi ta atomatik a task 8.12.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Tushen home na Gemini mai ɗorewa; gudanarwa ta sau ɗaya tana amfani da home na wucin gadi da aka ware.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin jinkiri a milliseconds kafin a kashe child na CLI da aka aika wa signal da ƙarfi.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da DATA_DIR da aka fayyace don tsallake environment file na data-directory na tsoho mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Iyakar lokacin HTTP na kowace gwaji (ms) don buƙatun CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) da CLI ke jira endpoint na lafiyar server kafin ya buga gargaɗin ƙarewar lokaci. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali Windows). Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan bincike na retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory na musamman don gano plugins na CLI (packages na `omniroute-cmd-*`). Yana komawa zuwa `~/.omniroute/plugins/` idan ba a saita ba. Na CLI kawai — ba ya taɓa isa ga scanner na plugins na ɓangaren server, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nunawa (sashe na 2). |

## 10. Haɗe-haɗen Wakili na Ciki & MCP

| Maɓalli                                         | Tsoho                                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL takamaimai don kayan aikin MCP/A2A su isa OmniRoute. Yana soke ganowa ta atomatik ta localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Manhajojin MCP/A2A                                          | Maɓallin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na maɓalli don danganta rajistar binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Na gado                                                     | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Adadin kwanakin da ake riƙe tarihin ayyukan A2A a rumbun bayanai na cikin gida kafin sharewar yau da kullum ta goge layi. Idan ba a saita ba, ba lamba ba ne, ko `<= 0`, zai koma `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Maɓallin kashe fasalin sa-ido na memory-hits na A2A. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake binciken dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa gaba ɗaya ga wani aiki; kowace ƙima daban (har da rashin saiti) tana barin sa a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna ƙarshen sabis na Issue Agent na offline/na cikin gida don tantance matsalolin da aka rikoda. A bar shi a kashe sai idan ana gudanar da tsarin ayyukan tantance bayanan da aka rikoda na cikin gida kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Iyakar lokaci (ms) ga gudu guda ɗaya na tantance bayanan da aka rikoda na Issue Agent. Ana iyakance shi zuwa matsakaicin ƙimar cikin gida; idan ba a saita ba ko ba ingantacce ba, zai koma tsohuwar ƙimar da aka gina a ciki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(mahallin da ke aiki)_                             | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahalli/bayani na yanayin nesa na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana soke mahallin da ke aiki a ma'ajiyar mahallai ta cikin gida. Daidai yake da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe backend na sarƙar maɓallan OS na `keytar` na zaɓi don bayanan shaidar mahallin CLI. Idan an kunna, bayanan shaidar suna ci gaba da kasancewa a yanayin `0600` na `config.json`, kuma CLI yana fitar da gargaɗin komawa madadin sau ɗaya; an tanade shi don aikin headless/container da aka yi da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Tilasta sarrafa damar shiga bisa scopes a kan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(duk)_                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes da waƙafi ya raba: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Matse bayanan kayan aikin MCP kafin sauya manifest zuwa jerin bayanai. Ƙimomin kashewa: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Kasafin dakatarwa (ms) don matakan MCP da ke jiran mai samarwa (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Matsakaicin adadin misalan fihirisar local-corpus da ake adanawa a ƙwaƙwalwa (LRU, ɗaya ga kowane tushen kundin adireshi da aka yi wa fihirisa). | `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar duba iyakar ƙimar mai samarwa da ƙa'idar amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗauko ƙa'idar OAuth a jere yayin daidaitawar rukuni; ana ɗauko haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don kauce wa cunkushe sabis na sama. `0` yana janyewa daga wannan (a lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗauko ƙa'idar sabis na sama a jere a kan hanyar binciken-farko/sa-ido ta kowace buƙata; yana raba kiran hanyar sadarwa masu gudana lokaci guda don kada asusu da yawa a kan IP ɗaya su cunkushe sabis na sama. An haɗa shi cikin masu ɗauko ƙa'idar Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (duk wuraren ɗauko bayanan biyu), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Har yanzu ba a rufe hanyar turawa ta gama-gari `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ba — ana bibiyarta dabam. | `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Adadin millisekan da injin da aka buɗe zai ci gaba da kasancewa ana tsallake shi kafin gwajin rabin-buɗewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ma'ajiyar tubalan CCR mai ɗorewa (#9061). Saita `false` domin ajiye tubalan a ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. | `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Yawan lura da system prompt kafin a ɗauke shi a matsayin daskararren tsayayyen prefix.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

Yana sarrafa bayyanar setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Hanyar kaucewa: ba da damar request body ya maye gurbin filin project na Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Manufar credits ta Google One AI: `off` ba ya taɓa saka credits, `retry` yana saka su sau ɗaya bayan kuskuren quota 429 da ya cancanta, kuma `always` yana saka su a request na farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Ba da damar request translator na Antigravity ya tsallake tsauraran ingantawar request-signature na CLI idan upstream ya ƙi signatures na gaske (yanayin debug/tsohon-CLI). Ƙima mara sifili tana kunna tsallakewar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Maye gurbin token-file path na Antigravity CLI (agy) don shigo da local login ta auto-detect.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Ta Ciki)

| Variable            | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                 | Bayani                                         |
| ------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | gano-kai-tsaye    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL na server don gadar auth ta CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Auth token don gadar CLI.                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | User ID don sessions na gadar CLI.             |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.            |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.             |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.           |

## 11. Takardun Shaida na Mai Ba da OAuth

Takardun shaida da aka gina don **ci gaban localhost**. Don tura nesa, yi rijistar naka a kowane na'urar wasan kwaikwayo na mai ba da sabis.

| Mai Canzawa                                 | Mai Ba da Sabis          | Bayanai                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :------------------------------------------ | :----------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic)  | Abokin ciniki na jama'a — babu buƙatar sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code              | Sake tura URI. Tsoho: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI           | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)          | Yana buƙatar daidaitawa `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)   | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)         | Zaɓi don maye gurbin ID na abokin ciniki na jama'a na Muse CLI. Bar shi ba a saita shi ba don amfani da abokin ciniki na jama'a da aka gina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)     | Yana buƙatar daidaitawa `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)     | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot           | Abokin ciniki na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot              | Zaɓi don maye gurbin ID na abokin ciniki na OAuth na GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Yana komawa ga tsohon ID na `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` lokacin da ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot           | Zaɓi don maye gurbin ID na haɗin gwiwar abokin ciniki na GitHub Copilot da aka aiko a cikin `Copilot-Integration-Id` da `Editor-Plugin-Version` headers. Yana komawa ga `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8)  | Maɓallin API da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani da shi lokacin da babu takardar shaidar kowane haɗi. Zaɓi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)         | Hanyar al'ada zuwa binary na Devin CLI (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ne ke warware shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | ID na abokin ciniki na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Yi rijistar app a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da sake tura URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
