Yana komawa ga `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Komawar gado don `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani da shi lokacin da aka cire bambancin `_DUO_`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Komawar gado don `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)        | Komawar gado don `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                    | An saita don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                    | Maɓallin API kai tsaye (yana kaucewa OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                    | ID na sararin aiki don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                    | Sunan laƙabi don `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                    | Sauya kundin tsarin Qoder CLI (zaman PAT mai zaman kansa, yana kaucewa shiga mai bincike).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web             | Alamar `tk` ta gaba don aikawa a matsayin `validated` akan `/api/chat`. Ana buƙata lokacin da Blackbox ke tilasta daidaita alama; in ba haka ba OmniRoute yana komawa ga UUID bazuwar. Duba batu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Tsaron gadar hangen nesa | URL na tushe mai jituwa da OpenAI don kiran gadar hangen nesa marasa Anthropic. Yana komawa zuwa tsohon yanayin OpenAI URL ko api.openai.com. Nuna zuwa OmniRoute's `/v1` mai zaman kansa ko kowane ƙarshen OpenAI-compat (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Batu #2232. Lokacin da URL ɗin shine OmniRoute's `/v1`, ƙaramin buƙatar bayanin yana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma yana tantancewa tare da ingantaccen takardar shaidar mai zaman kansa (`sk_omniroute` mai tsaro a yanayin gida, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) don haka `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ayyukan suna aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Tsaron gadar hangen nesa | Maɓallin API don URL ɗin da ke sama. Yana sauya kowane mai bayarwa OpenAI / Google env vars don kiran gadar hangen nesa marasa Anthropic. Samfuran Anthropic suna riƙe da hanyar maɓallin Anthropic ɗin su. Batu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Tsaron gadar hangen nesa | Yaya tsawon lokacin da aka adana sakamakon hanyar "babu ɗan takara mai amfani", a cikin ms (tsoho `30000`). Ƙimar mara inganci ko mara kyau suna komawa ga tsoho; `0` yana kashe cache mara kyau. Tushe: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri ID na Abokin ciniki na OAuth 2.0 (nau'i: "Aikace-aikacen yanar gizo")
> 3. Ƙara URL ɗin sabar ku a matsayin Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Sauya ƙimar takardar shaidar a cikin `.env`.

## 12. Sauya User-Agent na Mai Bayarwa

Sauya taken `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa kowane mai bayarwa na waje. Ajin tushe na executor ne ke ƙayyade wannan a lokacin aiki:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushe:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Sauyi | Ƙimar Asali | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon nau'in CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kai tsaye zuwa Anthropic a dukkan hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (OAuth na asali da CLIProxyAPI). A tsoho, executor yana sanya wa sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba laƙabi ta hanyar da za a iya maimaitawa (yana amfani da daidaitaccen taswirar Claude Code idan akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase), sannan ya mayar da su zuwa asali a cikin amsa ta amfani da `_toolNameMap`, domin kada a ƙi harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case a matsayin abokan hulɗa na ɓangare na uku da aka gane ta sawun yatsa. Don gyaran matsala kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ta sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Sauya nau'in abokin cinikin Codex ba tare da dogaro da cikakken zaren UA ba |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Sauya nau'in Claude Code da ake sanarwa ba tare da dogaro da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` ba. Anthropic tana taƙaita damar wasu samfura bisa wannan ƙimar (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Sauya ƙarin haruffa 3 da OmniRoute ke haɗawa da `cc_version=` a cikin ɓangaren lissafin kuɗin Claude. Ƙara shi tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — kulle nau'in kawai yana sanar da haɗin `version.revision` wanda babu wani binary na gaske da ke fitarwa. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Sauya nau'in Copilot CLI da ake sanarwa ba tare da dogaro da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` ba |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya samu sabuntawa |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin da Antigravity IDE ya samu sabuntawa |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya samu sabuntawa |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` (ID na jama'a) na social device-code na Kiro |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Zaɓi na shiga: cikakken tabbatar da CRC na kowane frame a rafin event na Kiro (don gyara rafukan da suka lalace) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya samu sabuntawa |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya samu sabuntawa |

> [!TIP]
> Za ka iya ƙara sauye-sauyen User-Agent ga **kowane** mai bayarwa ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor yana gina sunan env var kai tsaye.

## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara taken HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su dace da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusu alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinka.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Na Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana kula da jerin taken da jerin filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke kula da takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu da kansa.

---

## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saita su ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban don girkawar Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da dubawar allo.

Tsarin da ake ganewa: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (laƙabi na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)               |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (laƙabi na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                 |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na dindindin don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0 saboda runtime ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani_ a ƙasan wannan takarda don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su da ɓoyewa a SQLite kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings na `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` ba sa cajin maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana cike na farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa ba da `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (laƙabi `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin `gemini` na dashboard. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan OpenAI `input` guda N suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Ƙarewar Lokaci

Dukkan ƙimomi suna cikin **milliseƙondi**. Ana warware su a wuri guda a `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsarin Matakan Ƙarewar Lokaci

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (sauyin da ya shafi komai)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (kiran masu samar da sabis na waje, tsoho: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 0 = a kashe, shawara: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 1260000; 0 yana kashewa)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (yana kayyade iyakar tsawaita lokacin shiri ta atomatik, tsoho: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (wanda aka samo, tsoho: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 0 = a kashe)
```

| Sauyawa                                         | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Gajeriyar hanya ta gama-gari — tana maye gurbin tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar HTTP don kira zuwa masu samarwa na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bytes daga sama kafin a dakatar. Samfuran tunani mai zurfi ba safai suke dakatawa >90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa idan an karɓi bytes daga sama kuma ba ya dogara da `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. An samo shi daga `timeoutMs` mafi girma na kowane samfuri a cikin rajista (1200000, Codex), tare da ƙarin tazarar 60000, don kada a taɓa katse samfurin da aka ba shi damar amfani da cikakken kasafinsa a tsakiyar amsa. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(a kashe)_                                                       | Ko OmniRoute na iya fitar da layukan sharhin SSE na `:` (misali bugun-zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarin bayanan `x-omniroute-*` na ƙarshe). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa (#10524), saboda tsauraran abokan hulɗa masu dacewa da OpenAI suna yin JSON.parse ga kowane layin SSE kuma su lalace kan sharhin `:`; bugun-zuciyar `data:` ba ya shafuwa. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake shiga. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin karɓar farkon aukuwar SSE wadda ba ping ba ce. Yana gaji `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga mai daidaitawa na jiran aukuwar farko don manyan buƙatun rafawa masu yawan amfani da kayan aiki ko zurfin tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Maɓallin kashe gaggawa na dabarar `/goal`. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ganowa gaba ɗaya — ba za a taɓa ƙara wa'adin jiran shiri da dawo da rafi saboda jikin buƙata/headers ba, wanda ke rage yiwuwar abokin hulɗa ya sarrafa ƙara wa'adin ƙarewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga na jiran aukuwar farko don gudanawar wakilin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna dawo da rafi da wuri ta atomatik don gudanawar wakilin `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe zaɓin shiga na musamman ga manufa. Wannan zai iya ƘARA dawo da rafi ne kawai a kan tsohon saitin mai gudanarwa — ba ya taɓa soke bayyanannen zaɓin ficewa na saitunan `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan SSE marasa ma'auni na `codex.*` (misali `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke karya `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK tare da 502. A KUNNE yake ta tsohuwa (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Jigilar app-server ta Codex da ake shiga bisa zaɓi. Mahadar WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) ta sidecar na `codex app-server` na gida. Idan aka saita tare da token, ana bi da buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa sidecar maimakon HTTP Responses API. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Token na izini/bearer na cikin layi da ake gabatarwa ga app-server. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar fayil ɗin da ke ɗauke da token na izinin app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da zagayen app-server ke gudana a cikinsa. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar amincewa da ake miƙawa ga zagayen app-server (misali `never`, `on-request`). Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar sandbox da ake miƙawa ga zagayen app-server (misali `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ba, executor yana amfani da `workspace-write` ta tsohuwa (an ƙarfafa tsaro; a baya `danger-full-access`). Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amince da buƙatun amincewa na app-server da kansa ta atomatik (aiwatar da umarni/fayil/izini a kan host). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin buƙatun ta atomatik; kiran kayan aikin harness ba ya shafuwa (suna bi ta keɓantacciyar hanyar wucewa ta `item/tool/call`). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Maye gurbin kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar headers na amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran taken amsa kan buƙatun yawo (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan iyaka; takamaiman `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na mai bayarwa yana da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowane yunƙuri kai tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokaci ya ƙare, ana sake gwadawa sau ɗaya a kan sabon socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar da kuma ci gaba da halin da ake yi a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | Mafi ƙarancin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga yunƙuran kai tsaye ba tare da proxy ba waɗanda uwar garken da ake nufi ta ke ta sirri/LAN/loopback (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), domin kada fara aikin Ollama na asali daga sanyi ya yanke saboda tsohuwar iyakar 30s da ke sama. Yana aiki a matsayin mafi ƙarancin iyaka: lokacin jira na ainihi shi ne mafi girma tsakanin wannan ƙimar da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙimar da ba ta inganta ba tana cire mafi ƙarancin iyakar gida. Wannan ba ya shafar uwar garken nesa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Iyakar sama (ms) ga yunƙurin RETRY ta sabon socket bayan ƙarewar lokacin jiran fara amsa na yunƙurin da ya yi amfani da haɗin da aka tara a sama (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa nasa siginar wa'adi (jerin fifikon haɗin da aka warware/model/mai bayarwa/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`); wannan siginar ita ce ainihin iyaka kuma ita ce ke fara aiki da farko a hanyar da aka tsara, don haka wannan kariya ce mai yalwa maimakon tsayayyar iyaka — ba tare da ita ba, sake gwadawar tana amfani da gajeriyar taga iri ɗaya ta `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar yunƙurin da aka tara, tana kuma haifar da 504 ga lafiyayyun nau'ikan tunani masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa yarda ta yi ƙasa da tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar da ke sama ba; idan mai kira bai bayar da siginar wa'adi ko kaɗan ba, sake gwadawar tana barin tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar yadda yake. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar rashin aiki na socket na keep-alive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin ƙarewar proxy na sawun yatsan TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin zuwa byte na farko musamman a jikin amsar jigilar sawun yatsan TLS ta wreq-js; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` kaɗai ba zai iya gano jikin da ya tsaya cak ba saboda yana warwarewa da zarar taken sun iso (#12656). Ƙarewar lokaci tana soke mai karatun wreq sannan ta koma ga dispatcher kai tsaye/na proxy; `0` yana kashe mai sa ido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai, kuma kawai yayin da alamar fasalin `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` ke kunne (a tsohuwa a kashe take): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin amsar Responses mai yawo bayan taken ta (#13484). Yawon Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka shiru bayan wannan taga tsayawa ne: ana sanya asusun cikin hutun ɗan lokaci sannan buƙatar ta juya zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa na biyu yana sa ta gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da alamar tana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= a kashe)                                                   | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: yana iyakance jiran taken amsa daga sama kan kiran Responses mai yawo idan har yanzu akwai wani asusu — buƙatar da ke cikin layi tana daina jira ta ci gaba maimakon riƙe cikakken lokacin taken. Kiran Responses masu yawo kawai abin ya shafa (yawon Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka shiru a nan layin jira ne, ba samarwa ba); kammalawar tattaunawa da kira marasa yawo ba sa canzawa, kuma tagar tana rage iyakar fara fetch mai tasiri ne kawai, ba ta taɓa tsawaita ta ba. Ƙimar da ake ba da shawara lokacin kunnawa: `30000`. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: adadin lokutan da iyakar jiran taken da ke sama za ta iya matsawa zuwa asusu na gaba a kowace buƙata. Asusun ƙarshe da ya rage koyaushe yana riƙe da cikakken lokacin taken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: ajiye buƙatar tare da heartbeat bayan samun 429 na wucin gadi sau da yawa (ko sabon alamar matsin pool), sannan a sake kunna sashe guda mai iyaka wanda ya ƙunshi asusu har guda 3 a jere maimakon baza buƙatun ga dukan rukunin (#13924). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juya zuwa asusu na gaba daidai yadda yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: idan ƙwaƙwalwar ƙin proxy ta ware duk asusun da suka shirya bayan cooldown a sashen sake kunnawa na park-and-resume, a yi amfani da su duk da haka maimakon kada a sake kunna komai (#14851, ci gaba daga #14750). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: sashen sake kunnawa babu komai a wannan yanayin, kamar yadda yake kafin #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Tatttaunawa mai yawo kawai: idan jikin farko daga sama ya tsaya kafin samar da event mai amfani, a yi yunƙuri na biyu guda ɗaya mai iyaka ta hanyar routing iri ɗaya, da kasafin shirye-shirye iri ɗaya kuma ba tare da hukunta asusu ba. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: tsayawar jikin farko tana sa buƙatar ta gaza ba tare da sake gwadawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: bayan 429 daga mai bayarwa da aka ware bisa egress a kan asusun da ba shi da proxy ƙarƙashin mahallin pool na gama gari, a nemi connection pool ya bayar da wani memba don yunƙuri na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress ɗin. Yana tsara fifiko, ba ya cirewa: pool da ya ƙare yana ci gaba da halin yanzu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juya zuwa asusu na gaba daidai yadda yake a baya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: sauya hanyar fayil ɗin alamar matsin pool da ake karantawa kafin a ajiye (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tsoho `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Sabuwar alama tana ajiye ba tare da sake ƙirga ba; idan babu ko ta tsufa, sai a koma amfani da burst counter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin tsallaken proxy don buƙatun bridge na `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Nuna mai aiwatar da web-fetch na Firecrawl zuwa instance da aka karɓi bakuncinsa da kai (API key na zaɓi ne idan ba a cloud ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokaci ga kowace buƙata don mai aiwatar da web-fetch na Firecrawl.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Base URL na mai debo ƙididdigar `/usage` ta Tavily (Provider Limits); ana watsi da shi idan yana nuna `api.tavily.com`. Idan ba a saita ba, ana komawa ga base URL na haɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Jimillar iyakar lokacin buƙatar uwar garke don bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin aika response headers ta hanyar bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Iyakar lokacin zaman jiran keep-alive na bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Iyakar lokacin raw socket (0 = an kashe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin rangwame bayan SIGTERM/SIGINT kafin a tilasta fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Madadin da `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` ke amfani da shi idan ba a saita `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Iyakar lokaci (ms) don tsare-tsaren `validationRead` da `modelsProbe` a cikin `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Ƙara shi don endpoints masu jinkiri (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) domin hana kai-komo tsakanin active/error a dashboard. Ana komawa zuwa 8000ms idan ƙimar ba ta inganta ba (<1000) ko ba lamba ba ce.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin fetch ta musamman ga relay a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay da ya maƙale dole ne ya gaza kafin iyakar lokacin client/agent (~30s), domin masu kira su ga gazawa ta musamman ga relay maimakon iyakar lokacin upstream ta gama-gari. An iyakance shi zuwa `29000` domin koyaushe shi ne zai fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Jinkirin sake gwadawa na gama-gari don hanyoyin sake-gwadawa-sau-ɗaya na direct/relay/proxy a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken hard-deadline na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken hard-deadline na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Yana ƙara "Kana da ginannen binciken yanar gizo. Amsa tambayoyi kai tsaye ta amfani da sakamakon bincike." zuwa system message na mai kira (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — Perplexity yana bincike ko ta yaya, kuma jimlar tana bayyana a cikin amsoshi a matsayin meta-commentary ga coding clients. Saita `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don dawo da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken hard-deadline na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
