| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Iyakar lokacin buƙatar native wreq-js (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` yana ɗaga shi zuwa `180000` ga kowace buƙata don dogayen generations.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwamen cikakken hard-deadline na JS da ake ƙarawa a kan iyakar lokacin native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Tafkin burauzar na Playwright da ake rabawa don hirar web-cookie mai dogaro da burauzar (`browserPool.ts`); saita `off` don kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanyar zuwa binary na `obscura` da tafkin burauzar da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground suke amfani da shi a matsayin injin farko (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga PATH na tsarin idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Nuna zuwa Obscura da tuni yake aiki (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da wani; wannan module ba shi ne ke mallakar wannan process ɗin ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Tashar jiragen ruwa da aka ayyana kai tsaye don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar tashar jiragen ruwa marar amfani ta atomatik idan ba a saita ta ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Sanya buƙatar hirar web-cookie ta yi amfani da hanyar da ke dogaro da burauzar (`browserBackedChat.ts`); saita `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL na tushe don mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); a canza shi kawai don endpoints na mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken endpoint na hira don mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(ana gano shi ta atomatik)_                                      | Hanyar zuwa executable na Chrome/Edge na tsarin don shiga Adobe Firefly ta burauzar hulɗa (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); yana maye gurbin ganowa ta atomatik bisa kowane OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ standalone output_                                       | Saitin madadin lokacin build don kundin adireshin standalone output da matakin haɗa-wuri bayan build yake amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); na kayan aikin build ne, ba na runtime ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Ƙoƙarin combo target suna gādon lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar upstream da aka warware (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan shi ne ke samar da fetch default). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
combo defaults, ko provider override kawai don sa fallback na combo ya fi sauri; ƙimomin da suka haura
lokacin ƙarewar upstream na yanzu za a iyakance su zuwa lokacin ƙarewar upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` wani kasafin lokacin bango ne na dukkan combo gaba ɗaya a faɗin failover targets.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko saita `0` don ci gaba da maimaitawa ba tare da iyaka ba (tsarin dakatar da
makalewa na minti 10 da aka rubuta kai tsaye a cikin `comboPredicates.ts` har yanzu yana aiki).
Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan matakin kariya ga combo. Ka tabbatar `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi
`targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover ya ci gaba da samun lokaci bayan target na farko mai jinkiri.

### Iyakokin Circuit Breaker

Daidaita circuit breaker a matakin provider. Defaults suna nuna ƙimomin da aka faɗaɗa da ake amfani da su tun daga v3.6 don haɗi 500+.

| Maɓalli                                                       | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da OAuth kafin mai katsewar ya kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Lokacin sake saiti (ms) na mai katsewar mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samarwa masu amfani da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Lokacin sake saiti (ms) na mai katsewar mai samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samarwa na gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Lokacin sake saiti (ms) na mai katsewar mai samarwa na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa a cikin lokacin da aka kayyade kafin dukkan mai samar da OAuth ya shiga lokacin jiran dawowa.                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi na ƙirga gazawa (ms) ga masu samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: lokacin jiran dawowa (ms) bayan an kai iyakar mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samar da OAuth yana shiga DEGRADED bayan wannan adadin gazawa.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Matsakaicin ninkawar ƙara `resetTimeout` na mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samar da OAuth yana ƙara matakin jinkiri bayan wannan adadin zagayen buɗewa.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa a cikin lokacin da aka kayyade kafin dukkan mai samar da API-key ya shiga lokacin jiran dawowa.                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi na ƙirga gazawa (ms) ga masu samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: lokacin jiran dawowa (ms) bayan an kai iyakar mai samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samar da API-key yana shiga DEGRADED bayan wannan adadin gazawa.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Matsakaicin ninkawar ƙara `resetTimeout` na mai samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai samar da API-key yana ƙara matakin jinkiri bayan wannan adadin zagayen buɗewa.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa kafin dukkan mai samarwa na gida ya shiga lokacin jiran dawowa.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi na ƙirga gazawa (ms) ga masu samarwa na gida.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Mai katsewa na matakin mai samarwa: lokacin jiran dawowa (ms) bayan an kai iyakar mai samarwa na gida.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Lokacin jiran dawowa na kowane maɓalli (ms), wanda juyawar API-key mai maɓallai da yawa ke amfani da shi idan 429 daga sabis na sama bai ƙunshi `Retry-After` ba (#14573). Ƙimomin da ba su fi sifili ba ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba suna komawa ga ƙimar tsoho. |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Zurfin jinkirin sake gwadawa wanda a kansa ake ɗaukar mai samar da fil na ma'ajiyar mahallin a matsayin marar lafiya na dogon lokaci, sannan a cire fil ɗin domin sauyawa zuwa wani.                                                                          |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Taga ta hana yawan sauyawa (ms) wadda ke jure gajerun lokutan jiran dawowa na wucin gadi kafin cire fil na ma'ajiyar mahalli.                                                                                                                                 |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                       | Saitin Tsari                                                 |
| -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Ƙirƙirar lamba mai ɗaukar dogon lokaci**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (minti 15)                       |
| **Iyakance jimillar tsawon rayuwar kwarara** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (minti 21)                |
| **Gaggauta gazawa don API na samarwa**       | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                          |
| **Samfuran tunani mai tsawo**                | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (minti 5 tsakanin ɓangarori) |

## 16. Rajista

Tsarin rajista yana rubutawa zuwa stdout da kuma fayilolin rajista masu juyawa. Ana karanta dukkan saituna daga `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Sauyi                                     | Tsoho                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin rajista: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (mai sauƙin karantawa ga mutum) ko `json` (mai tsari).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajista zuwa fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Hanyar fayil ɗin rajista (dangane da tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Matsakaicin girman fayil kafin juyawa. Yana karɓar: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko bayit kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana fayilolin rajistar manhaja da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Matsakaicin adadin kwafin ajiyar fayilolin rajista da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Yawan kwanakin adana shigarwar rajistar buƙata/kira a cikin rumbun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar rajistar kira a cikin ma'ajiyar ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `call_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_          | Tilasta kunna ko kashe cikakken rajistar buƙata, tare da ƙetare saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (awa 1)          | Matsakaicin shekarun shigarwar rajistar buƙata mai aiki da ta zama marar mai ita kafin tsaftace ƙwaƙwalwar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Adana gutsattsarin kwarara a cikin kayayyakin pipeline lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Dole ne a zaɓi kunnawa (`true`) — a kashe yake ta tsohuwa don adana sararin faifai.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman kayan rajistar kiran pipeline a KB lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin adadin layuka a teburin SQLite na `proxy_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/fita da farkon asusu a cikin rajistocin console na `[ProxyEgress]`. Bayanan rajistar proxy na dashboard/rumbun bayanai suna riƙe da cikakkun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don yin rikodin `first_chunk_ms` (daga fara aikawa zuwa bayit na farko mai amfani na jiki) a layukan rajistar proxy. Yana naɗe kowane jikin upstream a cikin lazy passthrough stream sannan ya gyara layin da zarar bayit ɗin ya iso; a kashe yake ta tsohuwa domin martani su wuce ba tare da an taɓa su ba. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (minti 1)          | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin rajista mai aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsawon kirtani da ake riƙewa a cikin kayayyakin rajistar hira (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan jeri da ake riƙewa daga ƙarshen jeri lokacin gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin kafa-cikin-kafa kafin a gajarta payload na rajistar hira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Matsakaicin adadin maɓallan object da ake riƙewa a cikin payload na rajistar hira (0 = babu iyaka).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Girman dukkan jikin buƙata/martani (KB) kafin a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani kawai maimakon cikakken kwafi. Ƙara wannan idan dogayen tattaunawar wakili suna nuna alamar riƙe wuri maimakon ainihin saƙonni a dashboard.                                                                                                            |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Lokacin da yake true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` yana tsallake gajartawa bisa girma. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## 17. Inganta Amfani da Ƙwaƙwalwa

| Variable                      | Default                                                                                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _auto_ (bare metal); **`1024` a cikin Docker image**                                            | Iyakar V8 heap (MB) da aka **ba da shawara** don Docker/standalone. Idan ba a saita shi ba, ana daidaita shi ta atomatik (~35% na RAM na tsarin, tare da ƙuntatawa zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` shi ne mafi ƙarancin ƙima kawai idan ba za a iya karanta jimillar ƙwaƙwalwar ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), ana ƙara ƙimar da aka **bayyana kai tsaye** a matsayin `--max-old-space-size` kuma ita ce ke **rinjayar** tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS idan sun saɓa (V8 last-flag). Har yanzu `omniroute serve` yana fifita tutar heap da ke akwai a NODE_OPTIONS (#5238). Kada a saita su biyun da lambobi daban-daban — tsarin yana rubuta gargadi da ke ambaton ƙimomin biyu da wanda ya yi nasara. **Docker image na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka ba a taɓa gudanar da daidaitawar atomatik a cikinsa ba.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` tare da ƙarin sararin cgroup — duba [Jagorar Docker — RAM na lokacin aiki](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`   | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; a kashe idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ba) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Cikakken makarin process-RSS (MB) don ƙwaƙwalwar native/external wadda V8 `heapUsed` ba zai iya gani ba; idan an wuce shi, sabbin buƙatu suna samun 503 `resource_pressure`. Ba kasafai RSS ke raguwa bayan tashin gwauron zabi ba, don haka a haɗa shi da `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                                            | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar system prompt da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                                | Matsakaicin jimillar girman prompt cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (minti 5)                                                                              | TTL na shigarwar prompt cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                                           | Matsakaicin adadin amsoshin temperature=0 da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                                | Matsakaicin jimillar girman semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                                            | TTL na shigarwar semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                                             | Tushen local-corpus waɗanda ke riƙe da index mai aiki a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa lokaci guda (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: idan an kai iyaka, ana cire index na tushen da aka fi daɗewa ba a yi amfani da shi ba, sannan a sake gina shi a tambaya ta gaba. Ana ƙuntata shi zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa tsohuwar ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                                            | Matsakaicin adadin abubuwan stream na baya-bayan nan a cikin buffer na kallon kai-tsaye na Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                               | Sauya tsawon context na duniya baki ɗaya (tokens) ga kowane provider da ba shi da variable na `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Idan an saita shi, yana **maye gurbin** ƙimomin catalog, sauyin Dashboard da registry ga waɗannan providers; idan ba a saita shi ba, OmniRoute yana amfani da jerin hanyoyinsa na ciki, wanda mafita ta ƙarshe ita ce `128000`. Duba [Tsawon context na kowane provider](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                                           | Ƙarin sararin token da aka tanada yayin bin diddigin iyakokin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Tsawon context na kowane provider (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) yana kuma karanta
nau'i na **kowane provider** wanda ake gina sunansa daga ID na provider a lokacin aiki, don haka ba za a iya
jera shi layi bayan layi a teburin da ke sama ko a cikin `.env.example` ba.

**Suna.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, inda `<PROVIDER>` yake ID na mai samarwa da aka mayar da shi zuwa manyan haruffa, tare da
maye gurbin kowane harafi da ba ya cikin `A-Z` / `0-9` da `_`. ID na mai samarwar shi ne na asali bayan
warware laƙabi (ɓangaren `provider` na rubutun `provider/model` bayan an warware gajeren laƙabi).
Nodes na musamman masu dacewa da OpenAI ko Anthropic suna amfani da ID na node ɗinsu da aka samar, don haka
UUID yana cikin sunan variable ɗin. Ƙimar ita ce adadin tokens mai kyau na lamba cikakkiya; duk wani abu
dabam (fanko, `0`, mara kyau, wanda ba lamba ba) za a yi watsi da shi sannan warwarewar ta koma mataki na gaba.

| ID na mai samarwa                                           | Sunan variable                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Fifiko.** Ga haɗin `provider/model`, mataki na farko da ya samar da ƙima shi ne zai yi nasara:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` na wannan mai samarwar.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (sauyin muhalli na gaba ɗaya).
3. Girman taga na kowane model: sauyin context da aka adana (wanda aka saita a dashboard, ko kuma
   discovery reconciler ya liƙa ta atomatik, duba `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), in ba haka ba ƙimar
   catalog / models.dev da aka daidaita.
4. `defaultContextLength` na registry na mai samarwar.
5. Hasashe bisa sunan model (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` /
   `codex` 400000).
6. Tsoffin ƙimomi da aka gina a ciki na kowane mai samarwa, sannan `128000`.

Dukkan matakan muhalli biyu suna aiki ga **dukkan mai samarwar**: kowane model na wannan mai samarwar yana samun
iyaka iri ɗaya, kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen kowane model na dashboard. Idan model guda ɗaya kawai na mai samarwa
ke buƙatar taga daban, yi amfani da sauyin kowane model a dashboard maimakon haka.

**Iyakar aiki.** Wannan jerin matakai ne ke tafiyar da duba context-window a lokacin request (ƙin amincewar
`context_length_exceeded` / "Input exceeds context window" a cikin
`open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), kasafin prompt compression da iyakokin combo context. `context_length`
da `GET /v1/models` ke sanarwa har yanzu zai iya fitowa daga metadata na catalog ko registry,
kuma ba a tabbatar cewa zai nuna sauyin muhalli ba.

**Misali.** Mai samarwa wanda model ɗinsa ke karɓar taga mai tokens 1,050,000 alhali catalog yana bayar da rahoton
128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Sake kunna server bayan canza shi: ana karanta ƙimar daga muhallin process na server.
A kan **desktop app**, server yana gudana a matsayin child process kuma ba ya ganin system variable
da aka saita bayan an ƙaddamar da app; saka layin a cikin fayil ɗin `.env` da app ke lodawa (tsarin bincike yana cikin
[Jagorar Electron: Binciken fayil ɗin muhalli](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)),
sannan ka rufe app ɗin gaba ɗaya ka sake ƙaddamar da shi.

### Matsewa

| Variable                              | Tsohuwar ƙima | Bayani                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ba a saita ba | Amince da `.rtk/filters.json` na project ba tare da hash na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani da shi kawai a cikin local development mai sarrafawa. |

### Injin Memory (tsari na 21)

### Kudin event-loop na memory, skills, da sabunta token (#10349)

OmniRoute **process guda ɗaya ne na Node**. Cirowa/dawo da memory, shigar da skills, da sabunta token na mai samarwa suna gudana a kan **event loop ɗaya** da `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba worker thread ba ne.

| Aiki                    | Code                               | Tsohuwar ƙima                                       | Sarrafawar mai gudanarwa                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Cirowa / dawo da memory | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** na dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory**. Babu kill switch na env na daban bayan kashe fasalin a settings.                                                       |
| Shigar da skills        | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** na dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Saitunan sandbox da ke ƙasa suna taƙaita execution ne kawai bayan an riga an kunna injection. |
| Sabunta token           | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | A kunne ga masu samarwar OAuth/web da aka haɗa      | Cire haɗin mai samarwar ko bar tokens su ci gaba da kasancewa masu inganci; babu env na `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` a yanzu.                                  |

Idan `/healthz` yana yin jinkiri a kan na'ura da ba ta da wani aiki, fara da kashe memory + skills, sannan ka duba nauyin catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasaloli suna ba da dama a wuraren `await`, amma har yanzu suna gogayya kan thread guda ɗin.

Saitunan embedding layer, vector store da reranking na persistent memory subsystem (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Sauyi                                | Tsoho                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (minti 5)          | TTL na ma’ajiyar embedding ta cikin ƙwaƙwalwa (ga kowane sa hannun tushe/model/dim).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Matsakaicin shigarwar LRU da ake riƙewa a ma’ajiyar embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | ID na ma’ajiyar HF don pipeline na MiniLM na gida na `@huggingface/transformers` wanda sai an zaɓa (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | ID na ma’ajiyar HF don embedder na static potion/Model2Vec mai amfani da jadawalin bincike. Ana sauke shi ne kawai lokacin da ake buƙata zuwa kundin ma’ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Kundin da ake amfani da shi don adana fayilolin static potion model. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsohonsa yana ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Sauya URL na asali na Hugging Face Hub da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani da shi (misali, endpoint na madubi don saitunan da ba su da haɗin intanet).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Tsohon top-K da binciken vector na brute-force na `sqlite-vec` ke amfani da shi a cikin `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Ƙimar dindindin `k` ta Reciprocal Rank Fusion don haɗaɗɗen dawo da bayanai na FTS5 + vector (girke-girken sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Hanyar gwaji/binciken matsala a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): idan `true`, tana tilasta vector store ya koma `null` (tana kwaikwayon yanayin cloud/WASM ba tare da `sqlite-vec` ba), wanda ke rage dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa zuwa binciken kalmomin FTS5. A bar shi ba a saita ba a production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Maɓallin API na backend na Notion (wanda sanannen backend preset na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | URL na asali na API na Notion (ana iya sauya shi don madadin Notion da aka karɓi bakuncinsa da kai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Maɓallin API na backend na Obsidian Vault (wanda sanannen backend preset na `genericBackend.ts` ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | URL na asali na API na Obsidian Vault (ana iya sauya shi don vault na nesa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Babban makunnin lalacewar ƙwaƙwalwa mai nau’i na TV6. **Sai an zaɓa (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa)** — sweep ɗin yana **share** ƙwaƙwalwar da ta lalace. Idan yana kashe, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bayanan telemetry ne kawai kuma ba za a taɓa share komai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) wanda bayan wucewarsa ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba za ta lalace. `0` yana sa episodic ma ta zama kariyayyiya. Nau’ikan dawwamammu (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) kullum suna da kariya. Agogon lalacewa yana sake kafa ma’auninsa bisa `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar da aka shigar sau `>=` wannan adadin tana samun kariya daga lalacewa ba tare da la’akari da nau’inta ba. `0` yana kashe kariyar isa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (a kashe)               | Tazarar lokaci (daƙiƙoƙi) don sweep na lalacewa na lokaci-lokaci na zaɓi a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ba a saita ba = babu sweep na lokaci-lokaci. Sai an zaɓa sau biyu: yana kuma buƙatar `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(ba a saita ba)_           | ID na providers da waƙafi ya raba (ba tare da la’akari da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba) waɗanda ke karɓar saƙon `system` **kawai a index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Ga waɗannan, saka ƙwaƙwalwa a tsakiyar array cikin aminci ga cache ba shi da aminci a tattaunawar juyi da yawa, don haka maimakon haka ana haɗa/ƙara ƙwaƙwalwar a gaba a matsayin saƙon system na farko. Tsohon saiti ya ƙunshi `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; a faɗaɗa shi don endpoints masu dacewa da OpenAI da aka karɓi bakuncinsu da kai (misali Qwen3.5+/3.6) waɗanda chat template ɗinsu ke tilasta irin wannan ƙa’idar saƙon system guda ɗaya a farko. |

### Misalin Docker Mai Ƙarancin RAM

`128` na dashboard kawai ne. Coding agents a wannan heap suna samun `FATAL ERROR` yayin dogon `/v1/responses`. Kada a yi amfani da wannan misalin a matsayin gateway na Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## Mai Samar da Playground na PromptQL (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Gadar zaman GraphQL da aka sake gina ta hanyar nazarin tsarin prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Dukansu na zaɓi ne — saitunan tsoho suna nuni zuwa endpoints na playground na jama'a; a sauya su kawai don tura PromptQL mai zaman kansa/wani dabam.

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho                                                              | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                         | Bayani                                                                        |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don ayyukan taɗi/zama.                  |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | GraphQL endpoint da ake amfani da shi don neman ma'aunin kuɗin amfani/amfani. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint da ake amfani da shi don sabunta token gwargwadon iyawa.             |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) na binciken `thread_events` kafin lokaci ya ƙare.     |

## Mai Bayar da Adobe Firefly na Yanar Gizo (Ba na Hukuma ba/Gwaji)

Sabunta zama ta hanyar burauza don mai bayar da Adobe Firefly na yanar gizo
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — an daidaita duk ƙimomin tsohuwa don shigarwar
kwamfutar tebur ta yau da kullum.

> **An cire a #9255.** Tsohon tsarin tafiyar Chrome mai haɗin CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) an
> maye gurbinsa da sabis ɗin shiga ta burauza na Playwright, kuma saitunansa ba sa samuwa. Sauye-sauyen
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tare da
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a ko'ina cikin
> tushen lambar — saita su ba ya yin wani tasiri.

| Sauyi                                | Ƙimar Tsohuwa     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome marar fuskar gani na gaske (an san cewa ba ya aiki don ƙirƙirawa; don gano matsala kawai).                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Shiga/fita daga dumama burauza tun da wuri. `0` yana kashe dumama tun da wuri (farfaɗowa daga 408 a tsakiyar rukuni har yanzu yana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`               | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don kashe adana zaman Adobe Firefly a faifai.                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) da ake tilastawa tsakanin aika buƙatu a jere, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazara (ms) da ake ƙara bayan nasarar rukuni, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkiri na tushe (ms) kafin aika buƙatar ƙirƙirawa, yana maye gurbin ƙimar tsohuwa da aka gina a ciki.                                    |

## 19. Daidaita Model (Dev)

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tilasta kunna ko kashe daidaita farashin models.dev. Idan ba a saita ba = a bi Saituna > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **yana rinjayar DB** kuma yana tsallake daidaitawa ta lokaci-lokaci da binciken SQL/JSON na `getModelsDevPricing()` (don farfaɗowa idan dashboard ya maƙale a kan event loop ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` yana tilasta kunna daidaitawa. Ajiye/share farashi har yanzu suna kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda ba ya yin komai ƙarƙashin takurawar mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kundin model a lokacin haɓakawa, cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h)     | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazara (daƙiƙu) ga mai daidaita context-window mai gyara kansa (5004): yana liƙa windows da provider ya ayyana daga gano `/models` a matsayin sauye-sauyen `auto:discovery` idan sun bambanta da kundin. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (ba sabon fetch); ba ya taɓa maye gurbin sauye-sauyen `manual`.                                                                                                                                                                                         |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` na relays na ɓangare na uku ne kawai waɗanda ke karɓar abokan hulɗar Claude Code
kaɗai. OmniRoute yana sake rubuta buƙatu domin waɗannan relays su karɓe su. Idan kana son amfani da
Claude Code CLI kawai, ko kuma ba ka da tabbacin abin da waɗannan relays suke, ka bar wannan a kashe sannan ka ƙara mai samarwa na yau da kullum
wanda ya dace da Anthropic a maimakonsa.
