## 21. Lafiyar Proxy

| Variable                                          | Default                     | Source File                                                                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                  | Wa'adin ƙarewar binciken lafiya na gazawa-da-wuri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                         | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                   | Tsawon lokaci (a cikin sa'o'i) don ƙididdige matsakaicin jinkirin proxy masu takara a dabarar pool da aka inganta don jinkiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                  | TTL na cache na sakamakon binciken lafiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                  | TTL na cache don binciken lafiyar proxy da ya gaza. Bar wannan ya fi `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` gajarta domin proxy da lokacinsa ya ƙare na ɗan lokaci yayin cunkoson buƙatu su sake gwadawa da sauri ba tare da kashe gazawa-da-wuri ga proxy da suka mutu gaba ɗaya ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Saita zuwa `false` don kashe mai tsara binciken lafiyar proxy na bayan fage wanda ke bincikar proxy da aka yi wa rajista lokaci-lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Tazarar zagayen mai tsara binciken lafiya na bayan fage a cikin ms (mafi ƙanƙanta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                     | Tazarar gwaji a ms don proxies na edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), waɗanda gwaje-gwajensu ke tayar da serverless isolate mai kuɗin amfani (mafi ƙaranci `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Tazarar zagayen dawo da aiki a bayan fage a ms: sau nawa ake sake gwada proxies da a baya ba su da lafiya domin proxy da ya farfaɗo ya koma juyawa ba tare da sake kunnawa ba. Ƙimomin da ke ƙasa da `60000` za su koma ga ƙimar tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip`    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                      | Manufar gwajin isa da mai tsara lokaci da endpoint na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` ke amfani da ita. Nuna shi zuwa URL na ciki/mai masaukin kansa don guje wa tsohuwar manufa ta jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                        | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                      | Adadin gwaje-gwajen da ake fara su lokaci guda a kowace rukuni, waɗanda mai tsara lokaci da endpoint na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` ke amfani da su tare. Ana sa mafi ƙarancin ƙima ta zama 1 kuma ana iyakance ta zuwa 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                       | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                      | Jinkiri a ms tsakanin aika gwaje-gwaje biyu a cikin rukuni. Idan babu shi, duk rukuni zai tashi a lokaci guda kuma IP na fita da ake rabawa zai iya jawo takaita yawan buƙatu daga manufar. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe tazarar; an iyakance shi zuwa 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                              | Saita `"false"` don dakatar da gwada ainihin host na mai samarwa da aka sanya wa proxy (`GET /models`, babu API key) kuma a koyaushe a yi amfani da `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` a maimakonsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                     | Lokacin da yake `false` (tsoho), gwaje-gwajen isa na atomatik (mai tsara lokaci + maɓallin "Gwada Duka" na `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) **na karantawa kawai ne** kuma ba sa taɓa rubuta matsayin proxy — mai gudanarwa ne kaɗai ke saita mai aiki/mara aiki, don haka gwaji marar tabbas ba zai iya barin proxy da aka sanya a makale ba (#6246). Saita `true` don dawo da tsohuwar halayyar gwadawa-da-saitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Tutar fasalin shiga-da-son-rai (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ƙimar override ta DB a dashboard ce ke rinjaya). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana sake gwada zagayen streaming da aka fassara amma babu komai ta hanyar takardun shaida ta yau da kullum (har zuwa sake-gwaje `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX`) maimakon nuna 200 mara komai ko 502 mai abun ciki mara komai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Tutar fasalin shiga-da-son-rai (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ƙimar override ta DB a dashboard ce ke rinjaya). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana nuna lura da hanyoyin fita na pool na karantawa kawai a ƙarƙashin proxy pool a dashboard (IP na fita daban-daban, haɗe-haɗe da kuma mafi yawan waɗanda aka gani a bayan IP guda cikin sa'o'i 24 da suka gabata, daga log ɗin proxy). Ba a taɓa amfani da shi don routing ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Tutat alama ta zaɓi (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); rinjayen DB na dashboard ne ke da fifiko). `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana karɓar adireshin da aka lura da su masu ɗauke da kwanan wata waɗanda mai gudanarwa ya tura ga kowane memba na pool, sannan ya haɗa su da karatun journal don nunawa da tsara jerin pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                        | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                       | Tagar lura a cikin sa'o'i don layukan egress da mai gudanarwa ya tura (ana karantawa a kowane karatu; ba a buƙatar sake farawa). An iyakance ta zuwa 1..168; idan ƙimar ta fita daga iyaka, ba lamba ba ce, ko babu komai, za a koma 24 tare da gargaɗi guda ɗaya a dukkan process, ba tare da jefa kuskure ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Saita `true` don bai wa scheduler damar cire proxies ta atomatik bayan gazawa da dama a jere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                         | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Yawan gazawa a jere kafin scheduler ya cire proxy ta atomatik (lokacin da `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Saita `true` don bai wa scheduler damar kashe proxy a sauƙaƙe (matsayi `dead`, ba za a taɓa gogewa ba) bayan gazawa da dama a jere, maimakon cire shi. Wannan madadi ne mara lalata ga `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: nan take proxy ɗin zai daina shiga warwarewar pool/rotation (tacewar matsayin alive da warwarewar scope-pool ke amfani da ita tuni tana ware shi), kuma za a sake kunna shi ta atomatik da zarar ya fara cin gwajin probe. Yana amfani da ma'aunin `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER` iri ɗaya. Idan tutocin biyu suna `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ne ke da fifiko.                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Tsallakewa ta passive na zaɓi: idan `true`, scheduler zai tsallake live probe ga endpoint wanda zirga-zirgar production na kwanan nan ta riga ta tabbatar da ingancinsa (aƙalla nasara ɗaya kuma babu gazawar proxy da aka danganta masa a cikin tagar, an lura da provider guda ɗaya tak). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: ana yi wa kowane proxy gwajin probe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                   | Tagar zirga-zirgar production a cikin ms da tsallakewar passive ke dubawa (an iyakance zuwa `60000..599999`). Ta fi tazarar sweep ta minti 10 gajarta domin gazawar kwanan nan ta rinjaya maimakon a raunana tasirinta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                     | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                          | Ba wa tsarin OAuth da hanyoyin tabbatar da provider damar ƙetare pinned proxy su haɗu kai tsaye lokacin da binciken farko na iya isa ga proxy ya gaza. Tsarin fifiko mai aiki shi ne rinjayen Feature Flags DB > env var > tsohuwar ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)               | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                          | Tsohuwar kasafin lokacin **jiran-layi**: tsawon lokacin da buƙata za ta iya jiran gurbin mai samarwa kuma ta kasance a layi kafin a ƙi ta, ba tare da ta taɓa isa ga uwar sabar ba. Ana share mai ƙidayar lokaci da zarar aikin ya fara gudana — ana iyakance lokacin gudanawar daban ta `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Env yana saita tsohuwar ƙima ne kawai: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` da aka adana, sannan `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` na kowace haɗi, kowannensu yana rinjayar ta.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (minti 10)         | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                   | Matsakaicin tsawon lokacin da buƙatar da aka amince da ita za ta iya ci gaba da gudana kafin ajiyar iyakar ƙimar ta ƙare — an ware shi daga kasafin jiran-layi domin jinkirin fara ɗauko bayanai a mashigai marasa ƙaruwa kada ya ƙare saboda lokaci (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (an kashe)              | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                   | Iyakar karɓar shiga layi: a ƙi da 429 `queue_full` da zarar buƙatu masu wannan adadin sun riga sun kasance a layi. `0` = babu iyaka (tsohuwar ƙima).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_           | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                   | Tilasta kunna/kashe tsarin kariyar iyakar ƙima ta atomatik ba tare da la’akari da saitin Dashboard da aka adana ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don tilasta kunnawa, `false`/`0`/`off` don tilasta kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                            | Bin diddigin lokacin hutawar mai samarwa/haɗi na duniya tsakanin buƙatu, wanda sai an zaɓi shiga. A KASHE yake ta tsohuwa (yana cin karo da Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                      | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                            | Mafi ƙarancin lokacin hutawa (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗin da ya gaza. Yana ƙaruwa bisa ma’aunin ninki gwargwadon gazawa masu jere. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai idan `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                            | Iyakar mafi girman lokacin hutawa (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗin da ya gaza ko da menene. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai idan `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (shuka) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabara) | **Mene ne:** dawo da rafukan uwar sabar da aka yanke ba tare da mai amfani ya lura ba (tashar free-claude-code). Yana riƙe tagar buɗewar SSE har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ta yadda yankewar _kafin-tabbatarwa_ — wadda ke faruwa kafin kowane byte ya isa ga abokin hulɗa — za a sake buɗe ta kuma a sake gwadawa a ɓoye. **Lokacin kunnawa:** uwar sabar da ba ta da tabbas/wadda take yawan yankewa da byte 0 a farkon rafi; a bar shi a KASHE idan ba za ka iya ɗaukar ƙarin jinkirin har zuwa 750 ms kafin samun alama ta farko a kowane rafi ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`. Yana shuka saitin Resilience da aka adana; saitin Dashboard yana rinjaya da zarar an saita shi. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (seed) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (logic)   | **Mene ne:** ci gaba daga tsakiyar stream (Fase 4.4) — bayan yankewa ta _post-commit_ (bytes sun riga sun isa ga client), a sake yin request tare da rubutun da bai cika ba a matsayin prefill na assistant, sannan a haɗa suffix ɗin da ya ɓace. Don streams na plain-text masu dacewa da OpenAI kawai; ba ya taɓa farawa yayin da tool call ke gudana. **Lokacin kunnawa:** generations masu tsawo waɗanda ake yankewa a tsakiyar amsa, kuma ka yarda ɓangaren ƙarshen da aka dawo da shi ya iso gaba ɗaya lokaci guda maimakon token-by-token. Ba ya dogara da `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (risk profile daban). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                         |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`              | Watchdog na useful-output na active-stream wanda ake kunna bisa zaɓi. Yana gano streams da ke ci gaba da aika chunks amma suke ƙasa da ƙayyadadden rate na assistant-output; heartbeats, usage events, empty deltas, da matakan tool/reasoning ba sa bayyana kamar an samu ci gaba. Ya bambanta da timeouts na idle da hard-deadline.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Lokacin rangwame kafin a tantance throughput, an iyakance shi zuwa 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Window na useful-output mai motsi, an iyakance shi zuwa 1000–600000 ms; ana buƙatar cikakken window guda kafin abort.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Mafi ƙarancin rate na bytes na UTF-8 na assistant-output (conservative token proxy), an iyakance shi zuwa 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Mafi ƙarancin sample na useful-output wanda ba sifili ba da ake ɗauka a matsayin abin aunawa, an iyakance shi zuwa bytes 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Tazarar stagger (ms) tsakanin healthchecks na token na provider yayin farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                       | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Mafi ƙarancin jitter na bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin healthchecks na token na provider, don hana bursting (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Mafi girman jitter na bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin healthchecks na token na provider, don hana bursting (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                        | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Girman batch na concurrent-check don binciken token-healthcheck na lokacin farawa; manyan values suna bincika connections da yawa a lokaci guda, ƙananan values kuma suna rage burst load (Issue #7875, regression na #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                         | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                  | Yawan automatic retries kan responses na cooldown da suka shafi model kafin a mayar da error ga client.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                        | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                  | Matsakaicin tazarar jinkiri (dakikoki) tsakanin sake-gwajewa bayan lokacin jira. Ana iyakance shi da wannan ƙimar ba tare da la’akari da `Retry-After` na upstream ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`     | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                              | URL na proxy mai adana token na Headroom. Tsarin rayuwar dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) yana ƙaddamar da CLI na `headroom-ai` na gida a kan loopback ta tsohuwa; a sauya wannan kawai idan ana son nuna wani proxy na Docker sidecar na waje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Konstantocin daidaita dawo da stream

Ana daidaita halayyar riƙe bayanai don farfadowa ta hanyar konstantoci da aka rubuta kai tsaye a cikin
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), waɗanda aka nuna a nan don bayani —
canza su yana buƙatar gyaran lamba, ba env var ba:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin da ake riƙe taga ta buɗewar SSE
  domin a iya sake gwadawa idan aka katse da wuri kafin a tabbatar da kowane byte ga client.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — ƙayyadadden iyaka na taga da ake riƙewa; a tabbatar
  (flush + passthrough) da zarar adadin bytes ɗin nan ya taru, ba tare da la’akari da timer ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — matsakaicin adadin sake buɗe stream na upstream
  a ɓoye yayin da har yanzu ba a tabbatar da holdback ba.

> **Iyakar ƙimar sliding-window ta kowane provider (babu env var):** _fallback_ na iyakar ƙimar sliding-window
> ta kowane provider da aka ɗauko daga FCC yana cikin lambar
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, an haɗa shi ta hanyar
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma ana fitar da shi da **taswirar tsoho mara komai**
> kuma **ba shi da env var na operator** a halin yanzu — ana kunna shi ne kawai ta hanyar test hook /
> gyaran lamba. Da gangan ba a jera shi a teburin da ke sama ba. Limiter na relay na kowane `(token, IP)`
> wanda _yake_ da abin da za a daidaita shi ne `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

> [!CAUTION]
> Waɗannan variables suna samar da **cikakken output** kuma suna iya fallasa bayanai masu muhimmanci. **Kada a taɓa kunna su a production.**

| Variable                              | Tsoho                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Saita `1` don kunna cikakkun logs na Cursor executor (SSE chunks da aka decode, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Alias mai dacewa da tsofaffin nau'ikan `CURSOR_DEBUG`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Path na fayil na zaɓi wanda ke karɓar ɗanyen Cursor chunks da aka decode yayin da `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Lokacin jira kafin stream marar aiki ya ƙare (ms) ga Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                       | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ƙarin lokacin jira (ms) bayan composer kv_after_text soft terminator yayin da bytes suka rage a buffer — yana ba trailing exec_mcp tool call lokaci don kammala frame ɗinsa.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | a kunne (`!== "0"`)          | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tool-commit directive da ke sa composer-2.5 ya yi tool calls cikin aminci. Saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Rubutun system prompt da operator ya ayyana wanda ake liƙawa ga system message BAYAN translation (post-translation injection), yana isa ga hanyoyin codex/Responses da `/v1/messages`. Haka kuma ana amfani da shi a matsayin directive prefix da ake cirewa daga echoed system preamble blocks. Bar shi ba a saita ba don kashewa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (a kashe)                | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don cire echoed system-prompt preamble blocks daga farkon openai→claude stream. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — heuristics ɗin sun dace da tsarin rubutun turanci kuma suna canza response payload, saboda haka reply da ya fara da irin wannan sashe bisa halacci zai rasa shi.                                           |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Lokacin jira kafin fetch ɗin kowane hoto ya ƙare (ms) ga remote `image_url` vision input.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(ana bincikawa)_            | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Sauya hanyar binciken state DB na Cursor IDE da ake amfani da ita don gano nau'in IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(gano / kulle)_             | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | Agent CLI build id (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) don `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` a Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## 23. Haɗin GitHub

Ba masu amfani damar kai rahoton matsaloli kai tsaye daga Dashboard.

| Sauyi                 | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | Ma'ajiyar lamba a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | GitHub Personal Access Token mai izinin `issues:write`.                                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | tantance matsaloli / mataimakan wakilin cloud | Alamar samun damar GitHub ta gama-gari da ake amfani da ita a matsayin madadin `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, kuma mataimakan wakilin cloud a `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` suna amfani da ita. |

## Yanayin Turawa

Don jagorar SRE ta backend na relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, wurin sanya 9router idan aka kwatanta da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar komawa madadin yayin yawan zirga-zirga), duba [Dabarar Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mafi Ƙarancin Saitin Ci-gaban Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Docker na Yanayin Samarwa

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Muhalli Marar Haɗin Intanet / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0+)

Iyakoki da saitunan tsaro da ake amfani da su lokacin da tsarin Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) ke aiwatar da ayyukan atomatik da masu amfani suka ayyana a cikin muhalli mai keɓewa.

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Iyakar lokacin agogo ga kowace aiwatarwar lambar skill a cikin sandbox. Iyaka ce tabbatacciya; ana kashe duk abin da ya wuce ta.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(yana komawa ga `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Iyakar lokacin tsara aiwatar da skill a babban mataki. Saita shi sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da damar jerin ayyuka masu matakai da yawa.                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da skill zai iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya a cikin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da ake ɗauka daga kowace amsar HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tabbatacciyar iyaka ga haruffan stdout/stderr da ake dawo da su daga kiran sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar sadarwar fita daga cikin sandbox. Ta tsohuwa tana kasancewa **keɓantacciya** saboda tsaro.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(babu komai)_                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin container images da aka ware da waƙafi waɗanda aka amince da su don aiwatarwa a sandbox. Idan babu komai, ana amfani da tsohon ginannen zaɓi kawai.                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(tsohon ginannen zaɓi)_                       | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Container image da ake amfani da shi lokacin ƙirƙirar sandbox mai dogaro da Docker. Sauya shi don ɗaure amfani da keɓaɓɓen base image mai ƙarfafa tsaro.                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                         | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Container runtime don keɓe skills: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar runtime mafi dacewa da aka shigar bisa OS na host (Apple Container/OrbStack a macOS, WSL Container a Windows, Podman a Linux), sannan ya koma Docker idan babu. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kunna `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fitar bayanai daga kowace lambar skill. Haɗa shi da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da tsauraran manufofin `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a turawar da ake rabawa.

## 25. Ƙa'idojin Iyaka na Mai Bayarwa, Tunnels, Ajiyar Bayanai & Sauran Abubuwan Runtime

Wuraren samun bayanai na ƙa'idojin iyaka na mai bayarwa, tunnels na hanyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), rukunin egress na 1Proxy, ajiyar bayanan rumbun bayanai da ƙananan sauye-sauye na kowane fasali da matakin executor ko scripts ke ambata.

| Sauyi                                          | Tsoho                                                                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Jimlar haɗin Redis don tsarin bayan rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(uwar garken samarwa)_                                                     | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya uwar garken da ake amfani da ita don ɗauko ƙididdigar coding-plan na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | an samo shi daga uwar garken                                                | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya cikakken URL na ƙididdiga na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie na zaman console don mashigar ƙididdigar Token Plan ta sirri ta Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (maɓallin inference API ba zai iya karanta ta ba). Kwafi gabaɗayan taken buƙatar `Cookie` — yana ɗauke da `login_qwencloud_ticket` — daga kowane kiran `api.json` zuwa `cs-data.qwencloud.com` a home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Yana da muhimmancin tsaro kuma an iyakance shi ga zaman; an fi son amfani da filin Dashboard na `qwenCloudCookie` na kowace haɗuwa.                                                                                                |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Sauyin `sec_token` na hannu don mashigar console ta Token Plan. Yana da muhimmancin tsaro; idan ba a saita shi ba, mai ɗauko bayanan zai samo shi daga HTML na dashboard ta amfani da cookie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Sauya uwar garken mashiga don mai ɗauko ƙididdigar Token Plan ta sirri (misali, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` don console na Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL na dashboard da ake amfani da shi don samo `sec_token` daga HTML da aka shiga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Cookie na zaman console don API ɗin ƙididdigar Xiaomi MiMo Token Plan (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` — maɓallin inference API ba zai iya karanta ta ba).
