| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE`        | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Nau'in fayil na sirri na token ɗin sabis na ciki: hanyar zuwa fayil wanda abin da ke cikinsa bayan cire sararin farkonsa da ƙarshensa shi ne token ɗin. Ana duba shi ne kawai idan ba a saita canjin kai-tsaye ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED`            | `true`                                                                      | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | Yana wadatar da jerin masu samarwa na dashboard da ƙididdigar matsayin mako-mako na OpenRouter (#9324). Yana kunne ta tsohuwa; saita `false` don tsallake ɗauko bayanan bango gaba ɗaya (ba ya toshe aiki, kuma ba ya taɓa zama kuskure mai hatsari).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`             | `86400000` (awa 24)                                                         | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | TTL na cache don hoton-lokaci na ƙididdigar masu samarwa na OpenRouter, a cikin millisekan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_IMAGE`                        | `redis:7-alpine`                                                            | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Hoton Redis da mai ƙaddamar da Redis na dannawa 1 ke amfani da shi. Sauya shi zuwa `redis:8-alpine` ko madubin rajista mai zaman kansa yadda ake buƙata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QDRANT_HOST`                                  | `qdrant`                                                                    | _(bayanan martabar gungu na zaɓi)_                                        | Sunan mai masaukin sidecar na Qdrant lokacin da `--profile memory` ke aiki. Tsoho yana nuni zuwa sunan sabis na qdrant da ke cikin hanyar sadarwa; sauya shi don turawa ta waje. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai lokacin da `qdrantEnabled` yake `true` a cikin lamba (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts:108`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QDRANT_PORT`                                  | `6333`                                                                      | _(bayanan martabar gungu na zaɓi)_                                        | Tashar REST ta sidecar na Qdrant.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QDRANT_GRPC_PORT`                             | `6334`                                                                      | _(bayanan martabar gungu na zaɓi)_                                        | Tashar gRPC ta sidecar na Qdrant. Dakunan karatu na abokin hulɗa waɗanda suka fi son gRPC maimakon REST don ayyukan yawo suna amfani da ita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QDRANT_API_KEY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | _(bayanan martabar gungu na zaɓi)_                                        | Maɓallin API na zaɓi don Qdrant Cloud ko instans na cikin-gida mai buƙatar tantancewa. Babu komai → ba a aika da taken `api-key` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QDRANT_COLLECTION`                            | `omniroute-memory`                                                          | _(bayanan martabar gungu na zaɓi)_                                        | Sunan tarin don embeddings na ma’ajiyar tattaunawar OmniRoute. Ana ƙirƙira shi a fara aiki da girman `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_EMBEDDING_MODEL`                       | `text-embedding-3-small`                                                    | _(bayanan martabar gungu na zaɓi)_                                        | Sunan samfurin embedding na tsohuwa da ake rubutawa a metadata na tarin Qdrant. Ainihin embeddings ana samar da su ta duk wani mai samarwa da filin `embeddingModel` a cikin saitunan OmniRoute yake nuni zuwa gare shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`                           | `1536`                                                                      | _(bayanan gungu na zaɓi)_                                                 | Girman vector na embedding. Dole ne ya dace da samfurin da kake yin embedding da shi (text-embedding-3-small → 1536; ada-002 → 1536; nomic-embed-text → 768).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QDRANT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCT`                     | `128`                                                                       | _(bayanan gungu na zaɓi)_                                                 | Daidaiton lokacin gina fihirisar HNSW. Mafi girma = gini ya fi jinkiri, bincike ya fi sauri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED`                   | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Babban maɓalli na juyawar asusu da mai gudanarwa zai iya saita wa. Idan `false`, babu ɗaya daga cikin nau'ukan `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_*` da ke ƙasa da zai kunna komawa wani asusu (kashe babban maɓallin yana kuma hana nau'ukan 429/500/502 waɗanda aka kunna ta tsohuwa). Wannan yana bai wa manhajar gaba mai kulawa (misali, manhajar tebur ta VibeProxy) damar kwaikwayon ƙa'idojin juyawarta a injin komawa wani asusu na backend.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Lokacin dakatawa (daƙiƙoƙi) da ake sanya wa asusun da aka taƙaita yawan buƙatunsa idan upstream bai bayar da takamaiman alamar lokacin sake saiti ba. `0` = yi amfani da tsohon lokacin dakatawa na injin maimakon ƙayyadadden override.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kwafin zaɓin front-end na "kar a yi wa alama a matsayin wanda aka taƙaita yawan buƙatunsa ba tare da lokacin sake saiti ba".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna komawa wani asusu bisa kowane matsayi don kurakuran `429`. Idan `false` (kuma `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` ba zai ƙara kunna juyawar asusu ba, maimakon haka za a mayar da shi ga abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `429` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun. `1` (tsoho) yana juya shi nan take, yana kiyaye halayen da aka saba da su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙoƙi) da ake ƙirga kurakuran `429` a cikinta zuwa ga `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna komawa wani asusu bisa kowane matsayi don kurakuran sabar `5xx` (ban da `502`, wanda yake da nasa nau'in). Idan `false`, waɗannan kurakuran ba za su ƙara kunna juyawar asusu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `5xx` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun. `1` (tsoho) yana juya shi nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda ake ƙirga kurakuran `5xx` a cikinta zuwa `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna madadin aiki bisa kowane matsayi don kurakuran `502` (mummunar mashigar sadarwa). Idan `false` ne, kurakuran `502` ba za su ƙara jawo sauya asusu ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `502` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a sauya asusun. `1` (tsoho) yana sauya shi nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda ake ƙirga kurakuran `502` a cikinta zuwa `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Zaɓin shiga (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa): idan `true` ne, `400` na yau da kullum (mummunar buƙata) shi ma yana jawo sauya asusu. Wannan ƙari ne kawai — ba ya taɓa hana halin injin da yake akwai inda `400` mai ɗauke da rubutun iyakar ƙima/keɓaɓɓen kaso har yanzu yake komawa madadin ba tare da la’akari da wannan tutar ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `400` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a sauya asusun (ana duba wannan ne kawai idan `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tagar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙoƙi) wadda ake ƙirga kurakuran `400` a cikinta zuwa `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Mai Tsara Dumama Claude

Dumama mai aiki ta hanyar Cron ga haɗin Anthropic OAuth da aka zaɓa, domin a buɗe tagar iyakar ƙima ta awa 5 ta hanyar ƙaramar buƙata da aka tsara maimakon buƙata ta ainihi ta farko (#8848). Mai tsara aikin yana a kashe sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da ƙimar gaskiya **kuma** an yi wa haɗin alama a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi marar komai yana nufin ba za a dumama komai ba ko da an kunna canjin yanayin.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babban maɓallin kunna jadawalin warmup. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba tare da la’akari da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba, bayan cire sarari). Duk wata ƙima dabam, ko rashin saita shi, zai bar jadawalin a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Jimlar cron mai filaye biyar don zagayen warmup, ana kimanta ta a `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saitawa na Anthropic) ba tare da la’akari da agogon na’ura mai masaukin ba.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Yawan haɗin da ake yi wa warmup a layi ɗaya a kowane zagaye. Ana iyakance shi zuwa `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba za ta koma `3`.                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar warmup. Sauya shi kawai idan tsohon zaɓin ba ya samuwa a tsarinka; zaɓi samfurin mafi arha wanda har yanzu zai buɗe taga.                                                      |

### Zaman VNC na Shigarwa ta Burauza da Laƙabin Kundin Bayanai

Chromium+VNC da aka sanya cikin container wanda ake amfani da shi don tattara bayanan shaida na shigarwa ta burauza cikin hulɗa (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon laƙabin `DATA_DIR`. Dukkansu na zaɓi ne — tsoffin saitunan VNC suna nufin hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don hoton container na musamman, tashoshin sadarwa, ko daidaita zagayowar rayuwa.

| Sauyi                                 | Tsoho                                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar hoton Docker don container na shigarwar Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko kuma nuna wannan zuwa wani hoto na musamman.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Fayil ɗin binari na runtime ɗin container da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da container na VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka buɗe a cikin container.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin container.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin container.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin na’ura mai masaukin da ke ɗauke da bayanan martabar shigarwa ta burauza da aka adana.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (minti 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa’adin rashin aiki (ms) kafin a cire zaman VNC mara aiki.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin wa’adi na dole (ms) na tsawon rayuwar zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa’adin jira (ms) don burauzar da ke cikin container ta zama a shirye don CDP.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa’adin jira (ms) don tattara zaman/cookies da aka kama bayan an kammala shigarwa.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake miƙawa ga Chromium da ke cikin container.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓaɓɓiyar cibiyar sadarwar Docker da container na shigarwar VNC yake shiga (#12571) maimakon gadar tsoho, domin sauran containers ba za su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinsa ba.                                      |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                        | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Tsohon laƙabi** na `DATA_DIR`, ana duba shi ne kawai bayan ba a saita duka `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` ba. Yana gano ma’ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizo na Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Gwaji & Kayan Aikin E2E

Ana amfani da su ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. A bar duk
ƙimomin da ke ƙasa ba tare da saita su ba a turawar samarwa.

| Sauyi                                  | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin fara E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) don mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa ga `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirrin mai gudanarwa da ake shigarwa cikin muhallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiyar gida na lokaci-lokaci yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madaukin binciken lafiyar token na OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da wakafi waɗanda aka cire daga binciken sabunta token na rigakafi (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka keɓance na kashe binciken lafiya gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa masu gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa yayin da masu samarwa na cascade ke kasancewa masu amsawa kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Dakatar da hayaniyar bayanan binciken lafiya a stdout na Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin samarwa na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (ingantawa ta CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                               | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake hook ɗin cire OmniRoute (CI na amfani da shi don kiyaye `node_modules` yadda yake).                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                          | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) har sai uwar garken ya kasance lafiya kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen tsarin muhalli/yarjejeniya.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL da kayan aikin gwajin smoke na Electron ke tsammanin manhajar da aka shirya za ta samar.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimillar iyakar lokaci (ms) kafin kayan aikin gwajin smoke ya daina jira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tagar jira (ms) bayan shafin ya loda.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(ta atomatik)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Takamaiman hanyar zuwa executable na Electron da aka shirya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Kundin bayanai don gudanar da gwajin smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don adana kundin adireshin bayanan gwajin smoke bayan an gama gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don watsa rajistocin Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa ta amfani da kundin adireshin bayanai ɗaya, sannan a tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu ta zaɓi direban SQLite na asali.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sauya hanyar binary ta Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Bututun fassarar takardu

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Dukkan masu canji biyar ba a saita su ta tsohuwa ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai a kan na'urorin
da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Mai canji                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL na tushe mai dacewa da OpenAI don tsarin bayan fassara.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Alamar Bearer don tsarin bayan fassara (ba a taɓa rubuta ta a rajista ba).         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | ID na samfurin, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin milliseƙan.                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Buƙatun fassara masu gudana a layi ɗaya yayin aiki kan fayiloli / locales da yawa. |

## 27. Ciyarwar Radar (Karɓar Baƙunci da Kai)

Ƙarin zaɓi ne da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED ke sarrafawa (a kashe take ta tsohuwa — tutar
fasali ce da ake kunnawa ko kashewa ta hanyar Settings/DB, ba env var ba; duba
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Sauye-sauye huɗu na farko da ke ƙasa zaɓaɓɓun sauye-sauyen ƙimomi ne don ciyarwar da aka karɓi
baƙuncinta da kai ko aka yi mata fork da kuma hanyoyin supporter-key. Na biyar, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`,
mahaɗi ne na daban wanda ba shi da ƙimar tsohuwa zuwa keɓaɓɓen kwamitin ayyuka na mai shi.
Duba [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) don cikakken bayanin
module da kuma
[jerin kunnawa daga farko har ƙarshe da saitin da ake jagoranta](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Ba a saita mai karanta sanarwar Home/Changelog na gama-gari ta hanyar environment
variable ba, kuma baya dogaro da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED. Yana karanta URL na `news.json`
na ma'ajiyar jama'a da aka ayyana a `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` ta hanyar
GET kawai; ID na sanarwar da aka yi watsi da su suna ci gaba da kasancewa a ma'ajiyar gida ta burauza.

| Sauyi                         | Ƙimar Tsohuwa                                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL na tushe da ke bai ɗaya ga catalog, referrals, supporter-offers, da ciyarwar Intel waɗanda aka sanya wa hannu daban-daban. Sauya shi don nuna sabis da aka karɓi baƙuncinsa da kai ko aka yi masa fork. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(maɓallin tsohuwa da aka kafa)_             | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Maɓallin jama'a na Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ko PEM) da ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun ciyarwa daga ciyarwa ta musamman.                                                                        |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Ni mai ba da gudummawa ne" yake buɗewa (hanyar neman supporter-key ta GitHub OAuth).                                                                                          |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Tallafa wa aikin" yake buɗewa (shafin biyan kuɗi/tsare-tsare).                                                                                                                |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Keɓaɓɓen mahaɗin kwamitin ayyuka na mai shi kawai. Ana buƙatar HTTPS sai dai don HTTP loopback SSH forward; ƙimomin da ba a saita ba ko marasa inganci ba sa ƙirƙirar abin kewayawa.                        |

## Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani

Sauye-sauye masu zuwa sun bayyana a sigogin baya na `.env.example` amma **ba su da nassoshin runtime** a cikin codebase na yanzu. An cire su:

| Maɓalli                                                                                                                                                                         | Dalili                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Babu wani fayil ɗin tushe da yake karanta shi. SQLite ne kaɗai direban da ake tallafawa — babu buƙatar yin zaɓi.                                                                           |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Yana cikin tsoffin takardu/samfuran env amma ba a amfani da shi lokacin aiki. Zai iya dawowa a wata fasalar amfani da instances da yawa a nan gaba.                                        |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Ba a sanya iyakar wucin gadi ga girman ma'ajiyar bayanai ba.                                                                                            |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Mai yiwuwa an cire aikin tsaftace tsoffin fayiloli.                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba.                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba (Kimi Coding yana amfani da OAuth, ba binary na CLI ba).                                                               |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ba a ambace su a ko'ina cikin lambar tushe ba.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | An cire su a v3.8.0. Tsarin aiki ya daina karanta waɗannan maɓallan env — bayanan tantancewa suna fitowa daga Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / ma'ajiyar bayanai mai ɓoyewa. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Mai aiwatar da Cursor yana amfani da `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (duba §22).                                                                              |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Kiro yana cikin `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — maɓallin kunnawa/kashewar sa ba shi da wani tasiri.                                                              |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi tare da sauran samfuran maɓallan API na masu samarwa waɗanda ba a amfani da su a v3.8.0.                                                                                       |

### Gyaran Tsoffin Ƙimomi

| Maɓalli                   | Tsohuwar Ƙimar `.env.example` | Ainihin Tsohuwar Ƙimar Lamba | Gyara                                                                |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                          | `7`                          | ✅ An cire ƙimar mai ruɗarwa; an rubuta `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                          | `7`                          | ✅ An cire ƙimar mai ruɗarwa; an rubuta `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |

### Sake samar da saitin OpenCode (kayan aikin wucin gadi)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` don sake samar da `opencode.json`
mai ingantattun ƙimomin `limit.context` da `limit.output` waɗanda aka samo daga instance na
OmniRoute da ke aiki. Ba a buƙatar ko ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan don aiki na yau da kullum — script ɗin
kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Sauƙaƙe            | Tsoho                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Asalin URL na samfurin OmniRoute da za a tambaya game da `/v1/models`.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API don tantancewa a ƙarshen OmniRoute na `/v1/models`. Yana komawa ga `OPENCODE_API_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba.                                 |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API irin na OpenCode (`sk-...`) da ake rubutawa cikin `opencode.json` da aka sake samarwa. Yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba. |

### Kayan gwajin compression na offline (kayan aikin ad-hoc)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, wato CLI na kimanta compression na offline.
Ba a buƙatarsa don aiki na yau da kullum — kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Sauƙaƙe                      | Tsoho        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (fanko) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Shaidun JSON da mai gudanarwa ya bayar don mai samarwar da CLI na compression-eval na offline ke gwadawa (ana fassara su da `JSON.parse`). A bar shi ba a saita ba don gwajin busasshe. |

### Zaman Burauzar VNC

Ana amfani da su ta `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` don saita zaman Chromium marasa nuni masu amfani da Docker ga masu samar da sarrafa burauza ta atomatik. Duk na zaɓi ne — an nuna tsoffin ƙimomi a ƙasa.

| Sauƙaƙe                               | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar zuwa binary na Docker da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da kwantenonin VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hoton Docker na kwantenan VNC Chromium.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(tutocin ginannu)_            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin args na Chromium CLI da ake miƙawa burauzar da ke cikin kwantena.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC a cikin kwantena.                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantena.                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin bayanan martaba a cikin kwantena.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin ɓangaren host don bayanan martabar burauza masu ɗorewa.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin zaman rashin aiki (ms) kafin a girbe zaman VNC.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin tsawon zama (ms).                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin jiran shirye-shiryen burauza (ms).                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Wa'adin girbi/tsabtacewa (ms).                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenan ke shiga (#12571), ba tare da amfani da gada ta tsoho ba. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Kundin adana zaman zaren Notion na dindindin.                                                               |

### Tantancewar sabis na ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                                 | Tsoho | Bayani                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------- | ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –     | Token da aka saka kai tsaye don tantancewar sabis-zuwa-sabis na tsarin gudanarwa.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –     | Hanyar zuwa fayil mai ɗauke da token na sabis na ciki (an fi son wannan a cikin kwantena; yana rinjayar sauƙaƙen da aka saka kai tsaye). |

### Ƙididdigar mai samarwar OpenRouter

| Sauƙaƙe                             | Tsoho     | Bayani                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Saita zuwa `false` don tsallake ɗauko ƙididdigar kowane mai samarwa ta OpenRouter don inganta kundin bayanai. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL na ma'ajiyar wucin gadi (ms) don ƙididdigar masu samarwar OpenRouter da aka ɗauko.                        |

### Haɗin Redis da aka saka ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                     | Tsoho       | Bayani                                                                                |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adireshin ɗaurewa na sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa a ciki.                              |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Tashar jiragen ruwa ta sabis ɗin Redis da aka haɗa a ciki.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Sauyin da aka keɓance ga OmniRoute don adireshin ɗaurewa na Redis da aka haɗa a ciki. |

## 24. Ƙarin abubuwan sakin v3.8.50

An gabatar da waɗannan saitunan bayan hoton kwangilar muhalli na baya.

| Sauyawa                                     | Tsoho                      | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran gurbin karɓar hira mai nauyi kafin a mayar da `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa; ɗan gajeren jira mai iyaka yana jera cunkoson wakilai maimakon mayar da `503` nan take. `0` yana dawo da ƙin karɓa nan take.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Kasafin bytes da aka sa a layin jira don jiran karɓa: yana iyakance jimillar bytes na body da aka tara waɗanda aka ajiye a faɗin process ɗin, domin jiran kada ya ƙara girman heap (#4380). Jira da ya wuce kasafin yana karɓar `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon no-op ne tun daga #10110: an cire layukan karɓa na kowane session don amfani da kasafin guda ɗaya na faɗin process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar daidaitawa; amma ana yin watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon no-op ne tun daga #10110: an cire layukan karɓa na kowane session don amfani da kasafin guda ɗaya na faɗin process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar daidaitawa; amma ana yin watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (a kashe)              | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Layukan karɓa na kama-da-wane masu daidaitawa a runtime (#9654): babban maɓalli ne na ƙofar daidaitawa ta kowane tenant (tsari na 2). Ya bambanta da tsoffin sauye-sauyen layi na kowane connection da ke sama (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, no-ops tun daga #10110). Feature flag na Dashboard mai suna iri ɗaya; env var yana rinjayar override na dashboard; yana buƙatar sake farawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Yana iyakance tsawon lokacin da kiran run-now zai jira job da ke gudana kafin ya fara run ɗin da aka sa a layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | an kunna                   | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana sabunta yanayin IMS da haɗarin browser ta hanyar Chrome CDP sessions na kowane account; saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | an kunna                   | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana adana Adobe sessions da aka gyara a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`; saita `0` don yanayin memory kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Mafi ƙarancin tazara tsakanin aika buƙatun generate na Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Ƙarin lokacin shiru bayan kowane aikawa na Adobe na uku da ya yi nasara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Yanayin true-headless na debug kawai; Adobe colligo yawanci yana ƙin risk session da ya haifar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | ganowa ta atomatik         | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Cikakkiyar hanyar Chrome executable ta zaɓi wadda executors masu amfani da browser ke amfani da ita idan ganowa ta atomatik bisa platform ba ta wadatar ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token na BotFather wanda ke kunna inbound webhook kuma yake sanya hannu kan Mini App `initData`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Sirrin da aka raba wanda aka yi rajistarsa ta `setWebhook` kuma ake tabbatar da shi da header na `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` a kowane isarwar webhook. Ana buƙatarsa don hanyar webhook; idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin isarwar webhook da 503.                                                                                                                              |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model da ake amfani da shi don amsoshin hirar Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Override na URL na tushen Bot API don proxies ko sabobin Bot API da aka ɗauki nauyinsu da kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Timeout a cikin milliseconds don kiran Bot API masu fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Desktop release workflow yana amfani da shi don rage girman artifact da ake upload.                                                                                            |

| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`             | `60000`                    | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                                                     | Lokacin cooldown na kowane key a cikin milliseconds idan amsar `429` ba ta da alamar `Retry-After` mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
