| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(na ciki)_                | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Override na cikin gida na signal-handler na standalone-server; yawanci launcher ne ke saita shi kuma ya share shi maimakon operators su daidaita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Saitunan tsoho na gama-gari don headless browser da tool-tunnel mai fita. Ƙimomin Connection da aka saita a Dashboard suna da fifiko.

| Sauyi                                   | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium don gudanarwa ta npm, systemd da PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Madadin gama-gari don takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Wurin haɗin CDP na ciki; Docker yana amfani da sidecar a tashar `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Idan an saita shi, dole ne kowace buƙata zuwa CDP proxy sidecar ta aika wannan ƙima tare a cikin header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` (#13679). Idan babu ƙima, proxy zai tura buƙatun ba tare da tantancewa ba — a wannan yanayin keɓewar cibiyar sadarwar Compose `chatgpt-web-codex-net` ce kawai ke ba da kariya. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`. |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | ID na ramin OpenAI na bai ɗaya don zagayen kayan aikin Codex na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Runtime-Key na rami na bai ɗaya; kada a taɓa fitar da shi a cikin rajista.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ainihin sunan sabon ChatGPT-Custom-Connector da aka ƙirƙira don gadar MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Keɓantaccen kundin adireshi don yanayin browser, broker da rami.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Idan ƙimar ta kasance `1`, za a ɗauki hotunan binciken browser a kowane checkpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Cikakkiyar hanya ta zaɓi zuwa launcher binary mai ɗorewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Cikakkiyar hanya ta zaɓi zuwa Bun runtime binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Tsohon madadi don `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; sabbin saituna suna amfani da sunan hukuma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

---

## Gadar OmniConductor

Mai karɓar SSE mai dogon rai wanda ke kwaikwayon ayyukan cibiyar OmniConductor zuwa cikin A2A TaskManager na gida (`src/lib/conductor/`). Na zaɓi ne — gadar tana farawa ne kawai idan an saita `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Na ɓangaren sabar kawai: dole ne token ɗin cibiyar kada ya taɓa isa burauza.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Asali                  | Bayani                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL na asali na cibiyar OmniConductor (misali `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Idan ba a saita ba = an kashe gadar.                        |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Shaidar cibiyar don rafin SSE — ƙirƙiri takwarar nau'in `spokesperson` a cibiyar (`POST /v1/peers`, mai gudanarwa).                 |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Shaida don wakilta ayyuka masu shigowa daga A2A→cibiya (`POST /v1/tasks`); idan ba a saita ba, ana amfani da `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL na asali na sabis ɗin mai magana da yawun jama'a (Faro) da ke bayan wakilin tattaunawar dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).       |

### Tsarawa bisa la'akari da ƙayyadadden kaso

Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/services/combo.ts` da `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` don binciken kasafin token kafin buƙata. Na zaɓi ne — halayen turawa na asali ba ya canzawa idan ba a saita ba.

| Sauyi                              | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Asali                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Idan `1`, a tsallake haɗin da kasafin token ɗinsa na kowane taga (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, teburin `provider_quota_state`) ba zai iya ɗaukar kiyasin kuɗin buƙatar ba kafin aikawa. A ci gaba idan babu kasafin da aka saita.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(kunne)_ | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Daidaitawar mai ƙididdigar token na kariyar mahalli ta hanyar martani (#14931): EMA na `usage.prompt_tokens` / kiyasi ga kowane (mai bayarwa, samfuri, akwai kayan aiki), an iyakance shi zuwa 0.15–2.0, kuma a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. `off`, `0` ko `false` suna kashe rikodi da amfani. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`   | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Yana rijistar hanyoyin hoto na `xai-oauth` (`xao`) da `grok-cli`. Tutar fasali ta dashboard mai suna iri ɗaya; ƙimar maye gurbi ta DB tana rinjayar wannan ƙimar.                                                                                                                         |
